However, VLMs pre-trained on full images often struggle to capture local object details, limiting their effectiveness when applied to region-level detection. We present Decoupled Adaptivity Training (DAT), a self-supervised fine-tuning approach to improve VLMs for cooperative model-based object detection. Given a cooperative model consists of a closed-set detector and a VLM, we first construct a region-aware pseudo-labeled dataset using a pre-trained closed-set object detector, in which regions corresponding to novel objects may be present but remain unlabeled or mislabeled. We then fine-tune the visual backbone of the VLM in a decoupled manner, which enhances local feature alignment while preserving global semantic knowledge via weight interpolation. DAT is a plug-and-play module that requires no inference overhead and fine-tunes less than 0.8M parameters. Experiments on the COCO and LVIS datasets show that DAT consistently improves detection performance on both novel and known categories, establishing a new state of the art in cooperative open-vocabulary detection.

</details>


### [37] [AniMatrix: An Anime Video Generation Model that Thinks in Art, Not Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03652)
*Tencent HY Team*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AniMatrix是一个面向动画视频生成的模型，通过双通道条件机制和三步训练策略（重新定义正确性、覆盖物理先验、区分艺术与失败），解决了物理偏置模型在动画生成中会抹平艺术风格或崩溃的问题。它利用Production Knowledge System编码动画制作变量，采用风格-运动-变形课程学习，以及变形感知偏好优化，在专业动画师的五维度评估中四项排名第一，尤其在提示理解和艺术运动上显著超越Seedance-Pro 1.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型内化了物理现实先验，但动画故意违反物理规则（如运动模糊、冲击帧、Q版变形），且存在数千种共存的 artistic conventions，不存在单一的"动画物理"可供模型学习。物理偏置模型要么抹平动画的艺术性，要么在风格多样性下崩溃。

Method: 1) Production Knowledge System将动画编码为可控制作变量（风格、运动、镜头、VFX）的层次化分类体系，AniCaption从像素推断这些变量；2) 双路径注入：可训练标签编码器保留分类体系的字段-值结构（交叉注意力实现细粒度控制），冻结T5编码器处理自由文本（AdaLN调制实现全局约束）；3) 风格-运动-变形课程学习实现从近物理运动到完全动画表达的过渡；4) 变形感知偏好优化结合领域专用奖励模型，区分有意艺术变形与病态崩溃。

Result: 在专业动画师评估的五项制作维度中，AniMatrix四项排名第一，最大提升在于提示理解（+0.70，+22.4%）和艺术运动（+0.55，+16.9%），均超越Seedance-Pro 1.0。

Conclusion: AniMatrix通过结构化知识编码、渐进式课程学习和偏好优化，成功实现了面向艺术正确性而非物理正确性的动画视频生成，证明了从物理先验转向艺术先验的有效性。模型权重和推理代码将公开。

Abstract: Video generation models internalize physical realism as their prior. Anime deliberately violates physics: smears, impact frames, chibi shifts; and its thousands of coexisting artistic conventions yield no single "physics of anime" a model can absorb. Physics-biased models therefore flatten the artistry that defines the medium or collapse under its stylistic variance. We present AniMatrix, a video generation model that targets artistic rather than physical correctness through a dual-channel conditioning mechanism and a three-step transition: redefine correctness, override the physics prior, and distinguish art from failure. First, a Production Knowledge System encodes anime as a structured taxonomy of controllable production variables (Style, Motion, Camera, VFX), and AniCaption infers these variables from pixels as directorial directives. A trainable tag encoder preserves the field-value structure of this taxonomy while a frozen T5 encoder handles free-form narrative; dual-path injection (cross-attention for fine-grained control, AdaLN modulation for global enforcement) ensures categorical directives are never diluted by open-ended text. Second, a style-motion-deformation curriculum transitions the model from near-physical motion to full anime expressiveness. Third, deformation-aware preference optimization with a domain-specific reward model separates intentional artistry from pathological collapse. On an anime-specific human evaluation with five production dimensions scored by professional animators, AniMatrix ranks first on four of five, with the largest gains over Seedance-Pro 1.0 on Prompt Understanding (+0.70, +22.4 percent) and Artistic Motion (+0.55, +16.9 percent). We will publicly release the AniMatrix model weights and inference code.

</details>


### [38] [Unified Multimodal Visual Tracking with Dual Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03716)
*Lingyi Hong,Jinglun Li,Xinyu Zhou,Kaixun Jiang,Pinxue Guo,Zhaoyu Chen,Runze Li,Xingdong Sheng,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OneTrackerV2，一个统一的多模态视觉目标跟踪框架，通过Meta Merger和双专家混合（DMoE）机制，在单一端到端训练下实现RGB和RGB+X等多模态跟踪，在12个基准上达到SOTA性能，并具备高推理效率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法需要对每种模态分别训练模型或依赖预训练模型适配新模态，导致效率低、可扩展性差、可用性受限。亟需一个统一的端到端多模态跟踪框架。

Method: 提出OneTrackerV2框架：(1) Meta Merger将多模态信息嵌入统一空间，实现灵活融合和鲁棒性；(2) Dual Mixture-of-Experts (DMoE)包含T-MoE（建模时空关系用于跟踪）和M-MoE（嵌入多模态知识，解耦跨模态依赖并减少特征冲突）。所有模态共享架构和参数，单个端到端训练。

Result: 在5种RGB和RGB+X跟踪任务、12个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能，推理效率高。模型压缩后仍保持强性能，且在模态缺失场景下表现出显著的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: OneTrackerV2通过统一框架、共享参数和单次端到端训练，有效解决了多模态跟踪的可扩展性和效率问题，验证了跨模态统一建模的可行性。

Abstract: Multimodal visual object tracking can be divided into to several kinds of tasks (e.g. RGB and RGB+X tracking), based on the input modality. Existing methods often train separate models for each modality or rely on pretrained models to adapt to new modalities, which limits efficiency, scalability, and usability. Thus, we introduce OneTrackerV2, a unified multi-modal tracking framework that enables end-to-end training for any modality. We propose Meta Merger to embed multi-modal information into a unified space, allowing flexible modality fusion and robustness. We further introduce Dual Mixture-of-Experts (DMoE): T-MoE models spatio-temporal relations for tracking, while M-MoE embeds multi-modal knowledge, disentangling cross-modal dependencies and reducing feature conflicts. With a shared architecture, unified parameters, and a single end-to-end training, OneTrackerV2 achieves state-of-the-art performance across five RGB and RGB+X tracking tasks and 12 benchmarks, while maintaining high inference efficiency. Notably, even after model compression, OneTrackerV2 retains strong performance. Moreover, OneTrackerV2 demonstrates remarkable robustness under modality-missing scenarios.

</details>


### [39] [FluxFlow: Conservative Flow-Matching for Astronomical Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03749)
*Shuhong Liu,Xining Ge,Ziteng Cui,Liuzhuozheng Li,Gengjia Chang,Jun Liu,Ziying Gu,Dong Li,Xuangeng Chu,Lin Gu,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了FluxFlow，一种保守的像素空间流匹配框架，结合观测不确定性和源区域重要性权重进行训练，并采用无训练的维纳正则化测试时校正来抑制幻觉源，同时构建了包含19,500对真实共配准地面到空间图像对的DESI-HST数据集，在光度和科学精度上均优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面到空间的天文超分辨率面临像素采样分辨率和大气视宁度双重限制，现有方法依赖合成训练对无法捕捉真实大气统计特性，容易产生过度平滑重建或幻觉源。

Method: 提出FluxFlow框架，包含：(1) 保守的像素空间流匹配，在训练中融入观测不确定性和源区域重要性权重；(2) 无训练的维纳正则化测试时校正，抑制幻觉源同时保留恢复细节；(3) 构建DESI-HST数据集，包含19,500对真实地面-空间图像对。

Result: FluxFlow在光度和科学精度上均持续优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过融合观测不确定性和测试时正则化校正，FluxFlow有效解决了天文超分辨率中的幻觉抑制和细节保持问题，真实数据集的构建进一步验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Ground-to-space astronomical super-resolution requires recovering space-quality images from ground-based observations that are simultaneously limited by pixel sampling resolution and atmospheric seeing, which imposes a stochastic, spatially varying PSF that cannot be resolved through upsampling alone. Existing methods rely on synthetic training pairs that fail to capture real atmospheric statistics and are prone to either over-smoothed reconstructions or hallucination sources with no physical counterpart in the observed sky. We propose FluxFlow, a conservative pixel-space flow-matching framework that incorporates observation uncertainty and source-region importance weights during training, and a training-free Wiener-regularized test-time correction to suppress hallucination sources while preserving recovered detail. We further construct the DESI--HST Dataset, the large-scale real-world benchmark comprising 19,500 real co-registered ground-to-space image pairs with real atmospheric PSF variation. Experiments demonstrate that FluxFlow consistently outperforms existing baseline methods in both photometric and scientific accuracy.

</details>


### [40] [Before Forgetting, Learn to Remember: Revisiting Foundational Learning Failures in LVLM Unlearning Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03759)
*JuneHyoung Kwon,MiHyeon Kim,Eunju Lee,JungMin Yun,Byeonggeuk Lim,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReMem基准，旨在可靠评估大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）中多跳、多图像信息的记忆与遗忘情况，解决了现有基准中因模型无法有效记忆目标信息而导致遗忘评估不可靠的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM遗忘基准使用虚构身份进行评测，却忽视了关键的第一阶段失败：模型未能有效记忆目标信息，这使得后续遗忘评估不可靠。作者诊断出"欠记忆"和"多跳诅咒"是根本原因。

Method: 提出ReMem基准，通过原则性数据扩展、推理感知的QA对以及多样化视觉上下文来确保稳健的基础学习；同时提出Exposure指标，从模型内部概率分布量化信息擦除深度。

Result: 大量实验表明，ReMem为诊断LVLM中的学习与遗忘行为提供了一个严格且可信的框架。

Conclusion: ReMem有效解决了遗忘评估中因记忆不足导致的不可靠问题，并为LVLM的记忆与遗忘研究提供了更严谨的评估工具。

Abstract: While Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) offer powerful capabilities, they pose privacy risks by unintentionally memorizing sensitive personal information. Current unlearning benchmarks attempt to mitigate this using fictitious identities but overlook a critical stage 1 failure: models fail to effectively memorize target information initially, rendering subsequent unlearning evaluations unreliable. Diagnosing under-memorization and the multi-hop curse as root causes, we introduce ReMem, a Reliable Multi-hop and Multi-image Memorization Benchmark. ReMem ensures robust foundational learning through principled data scaling, reasoning-aware QA pairs, and diverse visual contexts. Additionally, we propose a novel Exposure metric to quantify the depth of information erasure from the model's internal probability distribution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReMem provides a rigorous and trustworthy framework for diagnosing both learning and unlearning behaviors in LVLMs.

</details>


### [41] [GeoTopoDiff: Learning Geometry--Topology Graph Priors through Boundary-Constrained Mixed Diffusion for Sparse-Slice 3D Porous Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03764)
*Yue Shi,Peng Wang,Mingzhe Yu,Yunlong Zhao,Li Liu,Gareth D Hatton,Yan Lyu,Liangxiu Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoTopoDiff提出了一种基于图扩散的框架，将扩散先验从体素空间迁移到混合图状态空间，同时建模连续孔隙几何和离散孔喉拓扑，通过稀疏CT切片实现大规模3D多孔微结构重建，在各项异性PTFE和枫丹白露砂岩上平均减少形态误差19.8%和传输误差36.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于体素的扩散模型在重建大规模3D多孔微结构时，需要完整CT扫描提供拓扑保真先验，导致在实际工业应用中吞吐量、拓扑保真度和视场之间存在固有权衡。

Method: 提出GeoTopoDiff框架，将扩散先验学习从体素空间迁移到混合图状态空间，同时建模连续孔隙几何与离散孔喉拓扑；引入拓扑感知的部分图先验，利用稀疏CT切片约束反向去噪过程。

Result: 在各项异性PTFE和枫丹白露砂岩实验中，GeoTopoDiff平均减少形态相关误差19.8%，拓扑敏感传输误差36.5%。

Conclusion: 混合图状态空间能促进扩散去噪过程，降低稀疏观测下的后验不确定性；所有模型和代码已开源以推动扩散模型在3D多孔微结构模拟领域的探索。

Abstract: Diffusion-based voxel prior modelling is challenging for the reconstruction of large-scale 3D porous microstructures. Due to the demanding requirements for simultaneously modelling both the continuous pore morphology and the discrete pore-throat topology, the diffusion models require fully observed CT scans to provide topology-faithful priors, which results in an inherent trade-off among throughput, topological fidelity, and field of view in practical industrial applications. We propose GeoTopoDiff, a graph diffusion-based framework for reconstructing 3D porous microstructures from sparse CT slices. GeoTopoDiff transfers the learning of diffusion priors from a voxel-based space to a mixed graph state space, which simultaneously encompasses continuous pore geometry and discrete pore-throat topology. A topology-aware partial graph prior from sparsely observed CT slices is introduced to constrain the reverse denoising process. Experiments on anisotropic PTFE and Fontainebleau sandstone show that GeoTopoDiff reduces morphology-related errors by 19.8% and topology-sensitive transport errors by 36.5% on average. Our findings suggest that the mixed graph state space promotes the diffusion denoising process to reduce posterior uncertainty under a sparse observations. All models and code have been made publicly available to facilitate the exploration of diffusion models in the field of 3D porous microstructures simulation.

</details>


### [42] [ReLeaf: Benchmarking Leaf Segmentation across Domains and Species](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03784)
*Robert Martinko,Daniel Steininger,Julia Simon,Andreas Trondl,Matthias Blaickner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了现有叶片实例分割数据集和模型，提出了一种适用于精准农业的最佳YOLO配置，并引入了一个包含23种植物的新基准数据集，发现跨领域泛化性能显著下降，表明需要更多样化的数据集来提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球粮食需求增长和气候压力增大，需要可持续、精准的农业实践。自动化的个体化植物处理依赖于细粒度视觉分析，但叶片级分割在评估作物健康、生长动态、产量潜力和局部胁迫症状方面具有重要价值，却尚未得到充分探索。进展受限于缺乏专用数据集（尤其是物种覆盖方面），以及缺乏对现代实例分割架构的系统评估。

Method: 调查现有数据并识别出四个可公开获取的叶片分割数据集，比较了单阶段、两阶段和基于Transformer的检测器，确定YOLO26模型配置为实际精准农业任务提供最佳权衡。通过半自动标注选定的CropAndWeed图像，创建了一个包含23种植物叶片级掩码的新基准数据集，并在所有数据集上进行了广泛的跨领域泛化实验。

Result: 在所有四个现有数据集上训练的模型在其对应测试集上达到83.9%的平均mAP50-95，在新基准上达到40.2%。跨领域泛化实验显示，不同植物物种和记录设置之间存在显著的性能下降，尤其仅使用实验室数据训练的模型泛化能力较差。

Conclusion: 改进的泛化性能证明了多样化的叶片分割数据集对鲁棒精准农业的必要性，强调了当前数据多样性的不足以及未来需要更全面的数据集覆盖。

Abstract: Rising global food demand and growing climate pressure increase the need for sustainable, precise agricultural practices. Automated, individualized plant treatment relies on fine-grained visual analysis, yet leaf-level segmentation remains underexplored despite its value for assessing crop health, growth dynamics, yield potential and localized stress symptoms. Progress is limited by a lack of dedicated datasets, especially regarding species coverage, and by the absence of systematic evaluations of modern instance-segmentation architectures for this task. We address these gaps by surveying current data and identifying four suitable, publicly available leaf-segmentation datasets. Using them, we compare one-stage, two-stage and Transformer-based detectors and identify a YOLO26 model configuration to provide the best trade-off for real-world precision-agriculture tasks. Extensive cross-domain generalization experiments reveal substantial performance drops across plant species and recording setups, especially for models trained solely on laboratory data. To strengthen data availability, we introduce a new benchmark dataset with leaf-level masks for 23 plant species, created via semi-automatic annotation of selected CropAndWeed images. A model trained on all four existing datasets achieves a mean mAP50-95 of 83.9% across their corresponding test sets and 40.2% on our new benchmark, demonstrating improved generalization and highlighting the need for diverse leaf-segmentation datasets in robust precision agriculture.

</details>

Method: 提出无监督域自适应框架，联合优化分类损失和基于再生核希尔伯特空间的最大均值差异损失，通过迁移学习对齐源域与目标域的特征分布，实现跨域泛化。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效减少对人工标注的依赖，增强模型在未标注医学数据集上的泛化能力；RKHS-MMD损失在医学影像分类中效果优于标准MMD，具备推进AI驱动医学诊断的潜力。

Abstract: Labeling medical images is a major bottleneck in the field of medical imaging, as it requires domain-specific expertise, and it gets further complicated due to variability across different medical centers and different imaging devices. Such heterogeneity introduces domain shifts and modality discrepancies, which limits the generalization of trained models. To address this important challenge, we propose an unsupervised domain adaptation framework that combines transfer learning with a Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space based Maximum Mean Discrepancy loss for the alignment of source and target domains. By jointly optimizing classification and RKHS-MMD losses, the methodology enhances generalization to unannotated medical datasets while diminishing reliance on manual annotation. Experimental evaluations presented on two chest X-ray datasets, which are obtained from different medical centers, show outstanding improvements over models trained without adaptation. Furthermore, we perform a comparative study to see that RKHS-MMD performs better than the standard Maximum Mean Discrepancy in reducing modality gap, emphasizing its effectiveness for medical image classification and also its strong capability in advanced AI-driven medical diagnostics.

*Quanxing Xu,Ling Zhou,Xian Zhong,Xiaohua Huang,Rubing Huang,Chia-Wen Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoVQD逻辑提示策略和CgRAG框架，通过融合链式推理与视觉问题分解来增强检索式VQA中多模态大语言模型的知识获取与推理能力，在多个基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索式VQA在开放域场景下，多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）对外部知识的获取不够准确和相关，缺乏结构化的推理引导，导致复杂跨域场景下的泛化性和可靠性不足。

Conclusion: 融合链式推理与视觉问题分解的逻辑提示策略能够有效引导检索，使MLLMs获取更准确相关的外部知识，从而在复杂跨域VQA任务中实现更好的泛化与可靠性。

Abstract: With advances in multimodal research and deep learning, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for a wide range of multimodal tasks. As a core problem in vision-language research, Visual Question Answering (VQA) has increasingly employed MLLMs to improve performance, particularly in open-domain settings where external knowledge is essential. In this work, we aim to further enhance retrieval-based VQA by more effectively integrating MLLMs with structured reasoning and knowledge acquisition. We introduce a logical prompting strategy that fuses Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning with Visual Question Decomposition (VQD), termed CoVQD, to guide retrieval toward more accurate and relevant knowledge for MLLM inference. Building on this idea, we propose a new framework, CoVQD-guided RAG (CgRAG), which enables MLLMs to access more comprehensive and coherent external knowledge while benefiting from structured visual-text reasoning guidance, thereby improving generalization and reliability in complex cross-domain VQA scenarios. Extensive experiments on E-VQA, InfoSeek, and OKVQA benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [45] [Multimodal Learning on Low-Quality Data with Conformal Predictive Self-Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03820)
*Xun Jiang,Yufan Gu,Disen Hu,Yuqing Hou,Yazhou Yao,Fumin Shen,Heng Tao Shen,Xing Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架CPSC，利用共形预测进行自校准，同时解决多模态学习中的模态不平衡和噪声腐败问题。核心是两个自校准模块：表示自校准（选择鲁棒特征分量融合）和梯度自校准（基于实例可靠性调整梯度流），并设计了共形预测器的自更新策略。在六个基准数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习中低质量数据主要表现为模态不平衡和噪声腐败，现有方法常孤立研究这两个问题，但它们共享一个共同根源——学习过程中对单个模态和实例可靠性的预测不确定性，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 提出CPSC框架，包含：(1) 表示自校准模块，将单模态特征分解为分量，通过共形预测器筛选最稳健的分量进行选择性融合；(2) 梯度自校准模块，基于实例级可靠性得分在反向传播中重新校准梯度流；(3) 共形预测器自更新策略，确保系统在训练过程中协同演化。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，涵盖不平衡和噪声两种设置，CPSC框架一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过共形预测实现的自校准机制能有效统一解决多模态学习中的模态不平衡和噪声腐败问题，提升模型对低质量数据的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal learning often grapples with the challenge of low-quality data, which predominantly manifests as two facets: modality imbalance and noisy corruption. While these issues are often studied in isolation, we argue that they share a common root in the predictive uncertainty towards the reliability of individual modalities and instances during learning. In this paper, we propose a unified framework, termed Conformal Predictive Self-Calibration (CPSC), which leverages conformal prediction to equip the model with the ability to perform self-guided calibration on-the-fly. The core of our proposed CPSC lies in a novel self-calibrating training loop that seamlessly integrates two key modules: (1) Representation Self-Calibration, which decomposes unimodal features into components, and selectively fuses the most robust ones identified by a conformal predictor to enhance feature resilience. (2) Gradient Self-Calibration, which recalibrates the gradient flow during backpropagation based on instance-wise reliability scores, steering the optimization towards more trustworthy directions. Furthermore, we also devise a self-update strategy for the conformal predictor to ensure the entire system co-evolves consistently throughout the training process. Extensive experiments on six benchmark datasets under both imbalanced and noisy settings demonstrate that our CPSC framework consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/XunCHN/CPSC.

</details>


### [46] [Identity-Consistent Multi-Pose Generation of Contactless Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03830)
*Zhiyu Pan,Xiongjun Guan,Jianjiang Feng,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IMPOSE是一个物理启发的多姿态接触式指纹生成框架，通过三阶段流程（潜扩散生成卷滚指纹身份、跨模态转换、3D物理模拟多姿态）合成保持身份一致性的多姿态非接触指纹样本，在UWA和PolyU CL2CB数据库上将跨模态匹配EER分别降至8.74%和2.26%，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非接触指纹识别因缺乏物理约束导致手指自由姿态引发严重的非线性几何畸变，造成非接触与接触式指纹之间存在跨模态域差距；现有显式几何校正或图像增强方法在极端姿态下效果脆弱。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 基于离散码本表示的潜扩散生成卷滚指纹身份；(2) 以Sauvola局部自适应二值化为身份锚点，将卷滚指纹跨模态翻译为非接触指纹；(3) 通过3D手指模型纹理映射与投影进行物理启发的多姿态模拟。

Result: 微调FDD在UWA上EER降至8.74%，PolyU CL2CB上降至2.26%；合成数据在DeepPrint、AFRNet等主流表征上均有持续增益；合成+真实数据混合策略取得最佳整体效果。

Conclusion: IMPOSE合成的多姿态非接触指纹样本能保持脊线拓扑级别的身份一致性及空间对齐，有效缩小跨模态域差距，显著提升非接触-接触指纹跨模态匹配性能。

Abstract: Contactless fingerprint recognition has gained increasing attention due to its advantages in hygiene and acquisition flexibility. However, the absence of physical contact constraints introduces severe nonlinear geometric distortions caused by free finger poses in 3D space, resulting in a substantial cross-modal domain gap between contactless and conventional contact-based fingerprints. Existing solutions largely rely on explicit geometric correction or image enhancement, which are fragile under extreme pose variations. In this paper, we propose Identity-Consistent Multi-Pose Generation of Contactless Fingerprints (IMPOSE), a physics-inspired framework that synthesizes identity-preserving, multi-pose contactless fingerprint samples to empower recognition models. IMPOSE consists of three stages: (1) rolled fingerprint identity generation via latent diffusion with discrete codebook representations, (2) cross-modal translation from rolled to contactless modality guided by Sauvola-based local adaptive binarization as an identity anchor, and (3) physics-based multi-pose simulation through 3D finger model texture mapping and projection. The generated samples maintain strict identity consistency at the ridge topology level and spatial alignment with standard fingerprint coordinate space. Extensive experiments on the UWA and PolyU CL2CB databases demonstrate that fine-tuning fixed-length dense descriptors (FDD) with IMPOSE-synthesized data achieves state-of-the-art cross-modal matching, reducing EER to 8.74% on UWA and 2.26% on PolyU CL2CB. Synthetic data also yields consistent gains across mainstream representations including DeepPrint and AFRNet, and the hybrid strategy combining synthetic and real data achieves the best overall results. The code and generated samples are available at https://github.com/Yu-Yy/IMPOSE.

</details>


### [47] [Conditions for well-posed color recovery in scattering media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03837)
*Grigory Solomatov,Derya Akkaynak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了散射介质中图像颜色恢复的病态性问题，证明了在理想高光谱相机条件下，利用图像中自然存在的跨像素关系（恢复模式）可以唯一确定解，为散射环境下的定量图像分析提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散射介质中的颜色恢复是一个基本逆问题，但由于解不唯一（多个解可对应相同观测）且无法控制预测误差，该问题本质上是病态的。现有方法缺乏对候选解空间的理解，亟需从理论上阐明在什么条件下该问题能够良态化。

Method: 论文首先识别出病态性的两个根源：(i) 光谱信号投影到像素强度造成的降维损失，(ii) 未知的介质参数。通过理论分析证明单纯改进传感器无法解决介质引起的畸变。进而引入"恢复模式"——图像中天然存在的跨像素关联——并结合理想高光谱相机假设，证明这些约束能将解空间限制为唯一候选。

Result: 给出了散射介质中颜色恢复问题变得良态的充分条件，即在理想高光谱相机条件下，利用恢复模式可以唯一确定颜色解。这一发现为基于第一性原理的视觉算法开辟了新途径。

Conclusion: 论文从第一性原理出发，揭示了散射介质颜色恢复良态化的充分条件，证明了跨像素关系（恢复模式）在唯一确定解中的核心作用，为散射环境下的定量图像分析奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Recovering scene color from images captured in scattering media is a fundamental inverse problem in optical imaging. Yet the problem is intrinsically ill-posed as multiple solutions can explain the same observation, and prediction error cannot be controlled without understanding the space of candidate solutions. Here, we present sufficient conditions under which color recovery in a scattering medium becomes well-posed. Observing that ill-posedness stems from (i) projection of spectral signals onto pixel intensities, and (ii) unknown medium parameters, we demonstrate that sensor improvements alone cannot resolve medium-induced distortions without additional constraints. We identify recovery patterns, cross-pixel relationships that naturally occur in images, and prove, for an ideal hyperspectral camera, that they restrict the solution to a unique candidate. This opens the door to a new class of vision algorithms grounded in first principles, enabling quantitative analysis of images in scattering environments.

</details>


### [48] [Parameter-Efficient Multi-View Proficiency Estimation: From Discriminative Classification to Generative Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03848)
*Edoardo Bianchi,Antonio Liotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了Ego-Exo4D多视角熟练度评估的三项最新工作：SkillFormer（参数高效选择性多视角融合）、PATS（基于基础运动局部密集采样的时序采样优化）和ProfVLM（将熟练度评估重构为条件语言生成，实现可解释反馈）。三者相较视频Transformer基线，参数量减少20倍、训练轮次减少3倍，推动了从封闭集分类向可解释生成反馈的范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动作识别仅关注"执行了什么动作"，而熟练度评估需要捕捉时序、平衡、身体力学和执行细节中的细微差异，且这些信息分布在多视角和短时序事件中，具有高度挑战性。

Method: SkillFormer采用参数高效判别式架构实现选择性多视角融合；PATS通过保留基础运动的局部密集时序片段改进时序采样；ProfVLM利用门控跨视角投影器和紧凑语言骨干将熟练度评估重构为条件语言生成任务，同时输出熟练度标签和专家级反馈。

Result: 在Ego-Exo4D上达到最先进精度，相较于视频Transformer基线，可训练参数减少20倍，训练轮次减少3倍。

Conclusion: 研究展示从封闭集分类向可解释生成反馈的范式转变，推动了高效、多视角系统的进化，关键方向包括选择性融合、熟练度感知采样和可操作生成反馈。

Abstract: Estimating how well a person performs an action, rather than which action is performed, is central to coaching, rehabilitation, and talent identification. This task is challenging because proficiency is encoded in subtle differences in timing, balance, body mechanics, and execution, often distributed across multiple views and short temporal events. We discuss three recent contributions to multi-view proficiency estimation on Ego-Exo4D. SkillFormer introduces a parameter-efficient discriminative architecture for selective multi-view fusion; PATS improves temporal sampling by preserving locally dense excerpts of fundamental movements; and ProfVLM reformulates proficiency estimation as conditional language generation, producing both a proficiency label and expert-style feedback through a gated cross-view projector and a compact language backbone. Together, these methods achieve state-of-the-art accuracy on Ego-Exo4D with up to 20x fewer trainable parameters and up to 3x fewer training epochs than video-transformer baselines, while moving from closed-set classification toward interpretable feedback generation. These results highlight a shift toward efficient, multi-view systems that combine selective fusion, proficiency-aware sampling, and actionable generative feedback.

</details>


### [49] [Stream-R1: Reliability-Perplexity Aware Reward Distillation for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03849)
*Bin Wu,Mengqi Huang,Shaojin Wu,Weinan Jia,Yuxin Wang,Zhendong Mao,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stream-R1提出了一种基于可靠性和困惑度感知的奖励蒸馏框架，通过自适应重加权蒸馏目标，在流式视频扩散模型的加速蒸馏中同时提升了视觉质量、运动质量和文本对齐三个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蒸馏方法（如DMD）对学生模型的所有rollout、帧和像素赋予同等权重，忽视了监督信号在inter-reliability（不同rollout的可靠性差异）和intra-perplexity（空间区域和时间帧对质量改进的不平等贡献）两个轴向上的方差，导致蒸馏质量受限。

Method: Stream-R1通过共享的奖励引导机制在rollout级别和时空元素级别自适应地重新加权蒸馏目标：1) Inter-Reliability层：利用预训练视频奖励分数的指数函数重新缩放每个rollout的损失；2) Intra-Perplexity层：反向传播奖励模型提取逐像素梯度显著性，分解为空间和时间权重，将优化压力集中在最有改进收益的区域和帧上；3) 自适应平衡机制防止任一质量维度主导优化。

Result: 在标准流式视频生成基准测试上，Stream-R1在视觉质量、运动质量和文本对齐三个维度上均取得了一致的改进，且无需修改架构或增加推理成本。

Conclusion: 通过自适应的奖励导向重加权机制，Stream-R1有效解决了蒸馏中监督信号可靠性差异的问题，为流式视频扩散模型的加速蒸馏提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Distillation-based acceleration has become foundational for making autoregressive streaming video diffusion models practical, with distribution matching distillation (DMD) as the de facto choice. Existing methods, however, train the student to match the teacher's output indiscriminately, treating every rollout, frame, and pixel as equally reliable supervision. We argue that this caps distilled quality, since it overlooks two complementary axes of variance in DMD supervision: Inter-Reliability across student rollouts whose supervision varies in reliability, and Intra-Perplexity across spatial regions and temporal frames that contribute unequally to where quality can still be improved. The objective thus conflates two questions under a uniform weight: whether to learn from each rollout, and where to concentrate optimization within it. To address this, we propose Stream-R1, a Reliability-Perplexity Aware Reward Distillation framework that adaptively reweights the distillation objective at both rollout and spatiotemporal-element levels through a single shared reward-guided mechanism. At the Inter-Reliability level, Stream-R1 rescales each rollout's loss by an exponential of a pretrained video reward score, so that rollouts with reliable supervision dominate optimization. At the Intra-Perplexity level, it back-propagates the same reward model to extract per-pixel gradient saliency, which is factored into spatial and temporal weights that concentrate optimization pressure on regions and frames where refinement yields the largest expected gain. An adaptive balancing mechanism prevents any single quality axis from dominating across visual quality, motion quality, and text alignment. Stream-R1 attains consistent improvements on all three dimensions over distillation baselines on standard streaming video generation benchmarks, without architectural modification or additional inference cost.

</details>


### [50] [A Deeper Dive into the Irreversibility of PolyProtect: Making Protected Face Templates Harder to Invert](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03857)
*Vedrana Krivokuća Hahn,Jérémy Maceiras,Sébastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文深入分析了PolyProtect（一种基于多元多项式的生物特征模板保护方法）的不可逆性，发现其易受基于余弦距离的数值求解器攻击。为此提出了一种密钥选择算法，通过优化多项式系数和指数来增强不可逆性，并证明该方法能有效平衡不可逆性与识别精度之间的权衡。同时发现嵌入元素的值域范围会影响精度，但可通过预处理归一化来改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PolyProtect方法的不可逆性分析不够深入，且其安全性与实用性之间的权衡缺乏有效的控制手段。

Method: 基于余弦距离的数值求解器进行逆向攻击分析；提出密钥选择算法优化多项式系数和指数以增强不可逆性；对嵌入进行归一化预处理以改善精度。

Result: 密钥选择算法能有效增强PolyProtected模板的不可逆性，并均衡不同重叠参数下的不可逆性水平；嵌入归一化可改善识别精度。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法能更好地控制不可逆性与精度的权衡，提升了PolyProtect的安全性。

Abstract: This work presents a deeper analysis of the "irreversibility" property of PolyProtect, a biometric template protection method initially proposed for securing face embeddings. PolyProtect transforms embeddings into protected templates via multivariate polynomials, whose coefficients and exponents are distinct for each subject enrolled in the face recognition system. A polynomial is applied to consecutive sets of elements from a given embedding, where the amount of overlap between the sets is a tunable parameter. We begin our irreversibility analysis by demonstrating that PolyProtected templates are easier to invert using a numerical solver based on cosine distance, as opposed to Euclidean distance (used in the earlier PolyProtect work). To make this inversion more difficult, we then propose a "key selection algorithm", which tries to choose "keys" (coefficients and exponents of the PolyProtect polynomial) that enhance the irreversibility of PolyProtected templates, compared to when the keys are purely random. Our experiments show that this algorithm is effective at generating PolyProtected templates that are significantly more difficult to invert, and that it approximately equalises the irreversibility of PolyProtected templates generated using different "overlap" parameters. This allows for better control of the irreversibility versus accuracy trade-off, known to exist across different overlaps. We also show that accuracy in the PolyProtected domain can be affected by the range in which the embedding elements lie, but that this can be improved by normalizing the embeddings prior to applying PolyProtect. This work is reproducible using our open-source code.

</details>


### [51] [DMGD: Train-Free Dataset Distillation with Semantic-Distribution Matching in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03877)
*Qichao Wang,Yunhong Lu,Hengyuan Cao,Junyi Zhang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双匹配引导的扩散模型框架（DMGD），通过训练无关的指导机制实现数据集蒸馏，在无需额外微调的情况下，在ImageNet子集上超越了需要微调的SOTA方法，平均准确率提升2.1%-5.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的数据集蒸馏方法通常需要额外的微调阶段，且有效的指导机制尚未被充分探索，限制了方法的效率和性能。

Method: 提出DMGD框架，包含：(1) 语义匹配——通过条件似然优化消除辅助分类器需求；(2) 动态引导机制——增强合成数据多样性同时保持语义对齐；(3) 基于最优传输的分布匹配——对齐目标分布结构；(4) 两种增强策略（分布近似匹配与贪婪渐进匹配）——以最小计算开销实现高效分布匹配指导。

Result: 在ImageNet-Woof、ImageNet-Nette和ImageNet-1K上，无需额外微调即分别超越需要微调的SOTA方法2.1%、5.4%和2.4%。

Conclusion: 所提出的训练无关指导机制能够有效解决扩散模型数据集蒸馏中的效率瓶颈，验证了无需微调即可达到甚至超越现有SOTA性能的可行性。

Abstract: Dataset distillation enables efficient training by distilling the information of large-scale datasets into significantly smaller synthetic datasets. Diffusion based paradigms have emerged in recent years, offering novel perspectives for dataset distillation. However, they typically necessitate additional fine-tuning stages, and effective guidance mechanisms remain underexplored. To address these limitations, we rethink diffusion based dataset distillation and propose a Dual Matching Guided Diffusion (DMGD) framework, centered on efficient training-free guidance. We first establish Semantic Matching via conditional likelihood optimization, eliminating the need for auxiliary classifiers. Furthermore, we propose a dynamic guidance mechanism that enhances the diversity of synthetic data while maintaining semantic alignment. Simultaneously, we introduce an optimal transport (OT) based Distribution Matching approach to further align with the target distribution structure. To ensure efficiency, we develop two enhanced strategies for diffusion based framework: Distribution Approximate Matching and Greedy Progressive Matching. These strategies enable effective distribution matching guidance with minimal computational overhead. Experimental results on ImageNet-Woof, ImageNet-Nette, and ImageNet-1K demonstrate that our training-free approach achieves significant improvements, outperforming state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods requiring additional fine-tuning by average accuracy gains of 2.1%, 5.4%, and 2.4%, respectively.

</details>


### [52] [Raising the Ceiling: Better Empirical Fixation Densities for Saliency Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03885)
*Susmit Agrawal,Jannis Hollman,Matthias Kümmerer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自适应带宽KDE、中心偏差、均匀分量和显著性模型的自适应混合模型，通过留一被试交叉验证优化每张图像的参数，显著提升了注视密度估计的准确性，中位数每幅图像的对数似然提升5-15%，AUC提升最高2个百分点，最难图像改进超过25%，并揭示了现有模型仍有显著改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，注视密度估计一直使用固定带宽各向同性高斯KDE，该方法几十年来未变。随着领域转向样本级评估（失败案例分析、逆向基准测试、逐图模型比较），可靠的单图像密度估计变得至关重要，现有方法已无法满足需求。

Method: 提出一个包含四个组分的混合模型：基于Abramson方法的自适应带宽KDE、中心偏差分量、均匀分量以及一个最先进的显著性模型。通过留一被试交叉验证对每张图像优化所有参数。

Result: 在多个基准测试上获得更高的观察者间一致性估计，中位数每幅图像对数似然提升5-15%，AUC提升高达2个百分点；最受影响的图像（即与失败案例分析最相关的图像）改进超过25%。利用改进的估计识别了现有SOTA模型的剩余失败案例。

Conclusion: 实证注视密度不应被视为固定的真值，而是随着方法改进而不断演化的估计。当前最先进模型仍有显著的改进空间。

Abstract: Empirical fixation densities, spatial distributions estimated from human eye-tracking data, are foundational to saliency benchmarking. They directly shape benchmark conclusions, leaderboard rankings, failure case analyses, and scientific claims about human visual behavior. Yet the standard estimation method, fixed-bandwidth isotropic Gaussian KDE, has gone essentially unchanged for decades. This matters now more than ever: as the field shifts toward sample-level evaluation (failure case analysis, inverse benchmarking, per-image model comparison), reliable per-image density estimates become critical. We propose a principled mixture model that combines an adaptive-bandwidth KDE based on Abramson's method, center bias and uniform components, and a state-of-the-art saliency model, to capture different spatial and semantic types of interobserver consistency, and optimize all parameters per image via leave-one-subject-out cross-validation. Our method yields substantially higher interobserver consistency estimates across multiple benchmarks, with median per-image gains of 5-15% in log-likelihood and up to 2 percentage points in AUC. For the most affected images -- precisely those most relevant to failure case analysis -- improvements exceed 25%. We leverage these improved estimates to identify and analyze remaining failure cases of state-of-the-art saliency models, demonstrating that significant headroom for model improvement remains. More broadly, our findings highlight that empirical fixation densities should not be treated as fixed ground truths but as evolving estimates that improve with better methodology.

</details>


### [53] [StateVLM: A State-Aware Vision-Language Model for Robotic Affordance Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03927)
*Xiaowen Sun,Matthias Kerzel,Mengdi Li,Xufeng Zhao,Paul Striker,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对视觉-语言模型（VLM）在机器人任务中数值推理能力不足的改进策略，通过引入辅助回归损失（ARL）进行微调，开发了StateVLM模型用于细粒度物体检测和状态定位，并创建了OSAR基准数据集，实验证明该方法有效提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型在机器人任务中表现出色，但在数值推理（特别是物体检测和状态定位）方面存在根本性局限，这一问题源于大语言模型固有的缺陷。

Method: 提出一种新的训练策略，在微调阶段利用框解码器输出计算辅助回归损失（ARL），同时推理时保留标准序列预测；基于此开发StateVLM模型；并构建了包含1,172个场景、7,746个物体的OSAR基准数据集。

Result: 在RefCOCO、RefCOCO+、RefCOCOg基准上，ARL使模型性能平均提升1.6%；在OSAR基准上，StateVLM+ARL平均提升5.2%，且在复杂的功能推理任务中显著增强了输出一致性。

Conclusion: 辅助回归损失（ARL）是一种有效的训练策略，能够显著提升VLM在物体检测、状态定位和功能推理等数值推理任务中的表现，StateVLM结合ARL实现了最先进的性能。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown remarkable performance in various robotic tasks, as they can perceive visual information and understand natural language instructions. However, when applied to robotics, VLMs remain subject to a fundamental limitation inherent in large language models (LLMs): they struggle with numerical reasoning, particularly in object detection and object-state localization. To explore numerical reasoning as a regression task in VLMs, we propose a novel training strategy to adapt VLMs for object detection and object-state localization. This approach leverages box decoder outputs to compute an Auxiliary Regression Loss (ARL) during fine-tuning, while preserving standard sequence prediction at inference. We leverage this training strategy to develop StateVLM (State-aware Vision-Language Model), a novel model designed to perceive and learn fine-grained object representations, including precise localization of objects and their states, as well as graspable regions. Due to the lack of a benchmark for object-state affordance reasoning, we introduce an open-source benchmark, Object State Affordance Reasoning (OSAR), which contains 1,172 scenes with 7,746 individual objects and corresponding bounding boxes. Comparative experiments on adapted benchmarks (RefCOCO, RefCOCO+, and \mbox{RefCOCOg}) demonstrate that ARL improves model performance by an average of 1.6\% compared to models without ARL. Experiments on the OSAR benchmark further support this finding, showing that StateVLM with ARL achieves an average of 5.2\% higher performance than models without ARL. In particular, ARL is also important for the complex task of affordance reasoning in OSAR, where it enhances the consistency of model outputs.

</details>


### [54] [A Benchmark for Interactive World Models with a Unified Action Generation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03941)
*Jianjie Fang,Yingshan Lei,Qin Wan,Ziyou Wang,Yuchao Huang,Yongyan Xu,Baining Zhao,Weichen Zhang,Chen Gao,Xinlei Chen,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: iWorld-Bench是一个用于评估世界模型物理交互能力（如距离感知、记忆等）的大规模基准测试，包含33万视频片段、6类任务和4.9k测试样本，对14个代表性模型进行了评估并揭示了关键局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏用于评估世界模型物理交互能力的大规模数据集和统一基准，现有模型在交互模态上存在差异，难以进行公平比较。

Method: 构建了包含33万视频片段的数据集，筛选2.1k高质量样本；设计动作生成框架（Action Generation Framework）统一评估；定义了6类任务（覆盖视觉生成、轨迹跟踪、记忆），生成4.9k测试样本。

Result: 对14个代表性世界模型进行了评估，识别出关键局限性，并提供了未来研究方向。

Conclusion: iWorld-Bench为世界模型的物理交互能力提供了统一的评估标准，有助于推动AGI研究发展。

Abstract: Achieving Artificial General Intelligence (AGI) requires agents that learn and interact adaptively, with interactive world models providing scalable environments for perception, reasoning, and action. Yet current research still lacks large-scale datasets and unified benchmarks to evaluate their physical interaction capabilities. To address this, we propose iWorld-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark for training and testing world models on interaction-related abilities such as distance perception and memory. We construct a diverse dataset with 330k video clips and select 2.1k high-quality samples covering varied perspectives, weather, and scenes. As existing world models differ in interaction modalities, we introduce an Action Generation Framework to unify evaluation and design six task types, generating 4.9k test samples. These tasks jointly assess model performance across visual generation, trajectory following, and memory. Evaluating 14 representative world models, we identify key limitations and provide insights for future research. The iWorld-Bench model leaderboard is publicly available at iWorld-Bench.com.

</details>


### [55] [Reservoir property image slices from the Groningen gas field for image translation and segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03942)
*Abdulrahman Al-Fakih,Nabil Sariah,Ardiansyah Koeshidayatullah,SanLinn I. Kaka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于Groningen静态地质模型的高分辨率储层属性图像数据集，包含相、孔隙度、渗透率和含水饱和度的对齐二维PNG图像，并附带可复现的处理工作流，用于支持地质图像分析方法的基准测试和跨域关系研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储层表征工作流日益依赖基于图像和机器学习/深度学习方法，但公开可用的地质图像数据集有限，难以支持可复现的基准测试。

Method: 从Groningen三维静态地质模型中生成储层属性图像切片，形成对齐的二维PNG图像（相、孔隙度、渗透率、含水饱和度）；提供完整的软件工作流用于数据增强、掩码生成、配对图像构建及基线实验。

Result: 产出了对齐的多属性储层图像数据集，以及可复现的处理工作流，将固定图像数据集与可复现流程分离。

Conclusion: 该资源为地球科学、储层建模和机器学习应用提供了透明且可复用的基准测试基础，支持地质图像分析方法的跨域关系研究。

Abstract: Reservoir characterization workflows increasingly rely on image-based and machine-learning/deep learning or even generative AI approaches, but openly available geological image datasets suitable for reproducible benchmarking remain limited. Here we describe a high-resolution dataset of reservoir-property image slices derived from the Groningen static geological model. The dataset contains aligned two-dimensional PNG images representing facies, porosity, permeability, and water saturation, generated from three-dimensional reservoir grids and prepared for downstream visualization, segmentation, and image-to-image translation tasks. In addition to the deposited original image corpus, we provide an archived software workflow for reproducing augmentation, mask generation, paired-image construction, and example baseline experiments. The resource is designed to support benchmarking of geological image analysis methods and the study of cross-domain relationships among reservoir properties. By separating the fixed image dataset from the reproducible processing workflow, this work provides a transparent foundation for reuse in geoscience, reservoir modeling, and machine-learning applications.

</details>


### [56] [UnAC: Adaptive Visual Prompting with Abstraction and Stepwise Checking for Complex Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03950)
*Yifan Wang,Yun Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UnAC（理解、抽象化、检查）多模态提示方法，通过自适应视觉提示策略、图像抽象化提示和逐步自检机制，增强LMMs（如GPT-4o、Gemini 1.5、GPT-4V）在复杂多模态任务中的多步推理能力，在MathVista、MM-Vet和MMMU三个基准上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LMMs在视觉感知方面表现强劲，但在需要多步推理的复杂视觉问题上仍不可靠，亟需一种能增强模型多步推理能力的方法。

Method: 1）自适应视觉提示策略：聚焦图像显著区域以提升理解与细节捕捉；2）图像抽象化提示：有效提取图像关键信息；3）逐步自检机制：通过验证每个分解后的子问题及其答案来改进推理过程。

Result: 在MathVista、MM-Vet和MMMU三个公开基准上进行了广泛实验，表明UnAC方法有效提升了LMMs在复杂多模态任务中的推理能力。

Conclusion: UnAC方法通过自适应视觉提示、图像抽象化和逐步自检的组合策略，显著增强了LMMs在需要多步推理的复杂多模态任务上的可靠性与表现。

Abstract: Although recent LMMs have become much stronger at visual perception, they remain unreliable on problems that require multi-step reasoning over visual evidence. In this paper, we present UnAC (Understanding, Abstracting, and Checking), a multimodal prompting method that strengthens reasoning for complex multimodal tasks in LMMs (e.g., GPT-4o, Gemini 1.5, and GPT-4V). To improve image understanding and capture fine details, we propose an adaptive visual prompting strategy that enables LMMs to focus on salient regions. We further design an image-abstraction prompt to effectively extract key information from images. In addition, we introduce a gradual self-checking scheme that improves reasoning by verifying each decomposed subquestion and its answer. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks-MathVista, MM-Vet, and MMMU.

</details>


### [57] [3D Human Face Reconstruction with 3DMM face model from RGB image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03996)
*Zhangnan Jiang,Zichen Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从单张RGB图像重建人脸3D模型的流水线，包括人脸检测、关键点检测、3DMM参数回归和软渲染，解决了粗粒度可变形人脸模型无法生成包含皱纹等细节的逼真数据的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN在图像处理领域表现优异，但需要大量标注数据训练；粗粒度可变形人脸模型合成的标注数据缺乏皱纹等细节，无法生成逼真的数据。

Method: 提出一个完整的流水线：人脸检测 → 关键点检测 → 3DMM模型参数回归 → 软渲染，从单张RGB图像重建3D人脸模型。

Result: 实现了从单张RGB图像到含细节的3D人脸模型的重建。

Conclusion: 该流水线能够利用CNN从单张RGB图像重建包含细节的3D人脸模型，克服了传统粗粒度模型缺乏真实感细节的问题。

Abstract: Nowadays as convolution neural networks demonstrate its powerful problem-solving ability in the area of image processing, efforts have been made to reconstruct detailed face shapes from 2D face images or videos. However, to make the full use of CNN, a large number of labeled data is required to train the network. Coarse morphable face model has been used to synthesize labeled data. However, it is hard for coarse morphable face models to generate photo-realistic data with detail such as wrinkles. In this project, we present a pipeline that reconstructs a human face 3D model from a single RGB image. The pipeline includes face detection, landmark detection, regression of 3DMM model parameters, and soft rendering. Mentor: Zhipeng Fan (Email: zf606@nyu.edu) Code Repository: https://github.com/SeVEnMY/3d-face- reconstruction Code Reference: https://github.com/sicxu/Deep3DFaceRecon pytorch

</details>


### [58] [Enhanced 3D Brain Tumor Segmentation Using Assorted Precision Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04008)
*Adwaitt Pandya,Ozioma C. Oguine,Harita Bhargava,Shrikant Zade*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文使用SegResNet架构和自动多精度训练方法进行脑肿瘤早期识别，在BraTS数据集上取得了整体肿瘤Dice 0.90、肿瘤核心0.84、增强肿瘤0.79的分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑肿瘤是影响各年龄段人群的严重疾病，早期识别对患者生存至关重要，因此需要开发高效准确的脑肿瘤分割方法。

Method: 采用SegResNet架构进行三维分割，使用自动多精度训练方法，以Dice损失函数作为优化目标，Dice系数作为评估指标。

Result: 整体肿瘤Dice系数0.90，肿瘤核心Dice系数0.84，增强肿瘤Dice系数0.79，平均Dice得分0.84。

Conclusion: SegResNet结合自动多精度训练在脑肿瘤分割任务上表现良好，能够有效区分肿瘤的不同子区域，为早期诊断提供了可行方案。

Abstract: A brain tumor is a medical disorder faced by individuals of all demographics. Medically, it is described as the spread of non-essential cells close to or throughout the brain. Symptoms of this ailment include headaches, seizures, and sensory changes. This research explores two main categories of brain tumors: benign and malignant. Benign spreads steadily, and malignant expresses growth, making it dangerous. Early identification of brain tumors is a crucial factor for the survival of patients. This research provides a state-of-the-art approach to the early identification of tumors within the brain. We implemented the SegResNet architecture, a widely adopted architecture for three-dimensional segmentation, and trained it using the automatic multi-precision method. We incorporated the dice loss function and dice metric for evaluating the model. We got a dice score of 0.84. For the tumor core, we got a dice score of 0.84; for the whole tumor, 0.90; and for the enhanced tumor, we got a score of 0.79.

</details>


### [59] [Large-Scale High-Quality 3D Gaussian Head Reconstruction from Multi-View Captures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04035)
*Evangelos Ntavelis,Sean Wu,Mohamad Shahbazi,Fabio Maninchedda,Dmitry Kostiaev,Artem Sevastopolsky,Vittorio Megaro,Trevor Phillips,Alejandro Blumentals,Shridhar Ravikumar,Mehak Gupta,Reinhard Knothe,Jeronimo Bayer,Matthias Vestner,Simon Schaefer,Thomas Etterlin,Christian Zimmermann,Mathias Deschler,Peter Kaufmann,Stefan Brugger,Sebastian Martin,Brian Amberg,Tom Runia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HeadsUp提出了一种可扩展的前馈方法，用于从大规模多摄像头设置中重建高质量的3D高斯头部模型。该方法采用编码器-解码器架构，将输入视图压缩为紧凑的潜在表示，然后解码为UV参数化的3D高斯函数，锚定在中性头部模板上。在包含10000多名受试者的内部数据集上训练和评估，实现了最先进的重建质量，无需测试时优化即可泛化到新的身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理大规模多摄像头设置时，难以在重建质量和计算效率之间取得良好平衡，且缺乏能够推广到新身份而无需测试时优化的可扩展解决方案。

Method: 采用编码器-解码器架构，将多视图输入压缩为紧凑的潜在表示，再解码为UV参数化的3D高斯函数，并锚定到中性头部模板。UV表示解耦了3D高斯数量与输入图像的数量和分辨率，支持高分辨率多视图训练。

Result: 在超过10000名受试者的数据集上实现了最先进的重建质量，能够泛化到未见过的身份，无需测试时优化。系统分析了模型在不同身份、视图和模型容量下的扩展行为。

Conclusion: 提出了一种可扩展的高质量3D头部重建方法，其紧凑的潜在空间支持新身份生成和表情驱动动画等下游应用。

Abstract: We propose HeadsUp, a scalable feed-forward method for reconstructing high-quality 3D Gaussian heads from large-scale multi-camera setups. Our method employs an efficient encoder-decoder architecture that compresses input views into a compact latent representation. This latent representation is then decoded into a set of UV-parameterized 3D Gaussians anchored to a neutral head template. This UV representation decouples the number of 3D Gaussians from the number and resolution of input images, enabling training with many high-resolution input views. We train and evaluate our model on an internal dataset with more than 10,000 subjects, which is an order of magnitude larger than existing multi-view human head datasets. HeadsUp achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction quality and generalizes to novel identities without test-time optimization. We extensively analyze the scaling behavior of our model across identities, views, and model capacity, revealing practical insights for quality-compute trade-offs. Finally, we highlight the strength of our latent space by showcasing two downstream applications: generating novel 3D identities and animating the 3D heads with expression blendshapes.

</details>


### [60] [Large Language Models are Universal Reasoners for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04040)
*Sucheng Ren,Chen Chen,Zhenbang Wang,Liangchen Song,Xiangxin Zhu,Alan Yuille,Liang-Chieh Chen,Jiasen Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UniReasoner框架，利用LLM作为通用推理器，通过生成视觉草稿和自我批评文本评估，将LLM的理解能力转化为扩散模型的生成指导，从而弥合文本到图像生成中的理解-生成差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管文本到图像生成系统在架构上实现了统一（LLM骨干同时处理视觉理解和生成），但它们经常无法在合成过程中忠实地对齐复杂提示，尽管它们在验证图像是否满足相同提示时非常准确。作者将这种不一致形式化为"理解-生成差距"，并希望利用LLM自身的理解优势来改善生成质量。

Method: UniReasoner包含三个阶段：(1) LLM首先根据提示生成由离散视觉token组成的粗略视觉草稿；(2) LLM对草稿进行自我批评，评估其与提示的一致性，生成有根据的文本评估，指出需要修正的问题；(3) 扩散模型以提示、视觉草稿和评估为联合条件进行生成，每个信号弥补另一个的不足：草稿提供场景级锚点减少文本条件的欠规格化，评估将验证转化为可操作的约束以纠正遗漏、幻觉和关系错误。

Result: 在相同扩散骨干下，UniReasoner提升了组合对齐和语义忠实度，同时保持了图像质量，有效缩小了理解-生成差距。

Conclusion: UniReasoner展示了利用LLM推理能力弥合理解-生成差距的实用途径，验证了将LLM的理解优势通过显式纠正信号转化为生成指导的有效性。

Abstract: Text-to-image generation has advanced rapidly with diffusion models, progressing from CLIP and T5 conditioning to unified systems where a single LLM backbone handles both visual understanding and generation. Despite the architectural unification, these systems frequently fail to faithfully align complex prompts during synthesis, even though they remain highly accurate at verifying whether an image satisfies those same prompts. We formalize this as the \emph{understanding-generation gap} and propose UniReasoner, a framework that leverages the LLM as a universal reasoner to convert its understanding strength into direct generation guidance. Given a prompt, the LLM first produces a coarse visual draft composed of discrete vision tokens. It then performs a self-critique by evaluating the draft for prompt consistency, producing a grounded textual evaluation that pinpoints what needs to be corrected. Finally, a diffusion model is conditioned jointly on the prompt, the visual draft, and the evaluation, ensuring that generation is guided by explicit corrective signals. Each signal addresses a limitation of the other: the draft provides a concrete, scene-level anchor that reduces under-specification in text-only conditioning, while the evaluation turns verification into grounded, actionable constraints that correct omissions, hallucinations, and relational errors. Experiments show that UniReasoner improves compositional alignment and semantic faithfulness under the same diffusion backbone while maintaining image quality, demonstrating a practical way to exploit LLM reasoning to close the understanding-generation gap.

</details>


### [61] [Audio-Visual Intelligence in Large Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04045)
*You Qin,Kai Liu,Shengqiong Wu,Kai Wang,Shijian Deng,Yapeng Tian,Junbin Xiao,Yazhou Xing,Yinghao Ma,Bobo Li,Roger Zimmermann,Lei Cui,Furu Wei,Jiebo Luo,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次以大型基础模型为视角，对音频-视觉智能（AVI）领域进行了全面综述，建立了统一的分类体系，涵盖理解、生成和交互三大任务类别，并系统梳理了方法论基础、数据集、基准和评估指标，旨在为未来大规模AVI研究提供基础性参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AVI领域在大型基础模型时代取得了快速进展（如Meta MovieGen、Google Veo-3等），但文献呈现碎片化状态，任务多样、分类体系不一致、评估实践异质，阻碍了系统性的比较和知识整合。因此需要一篇统一的综述来整合该领域。

Method: 建立统一的AVI任务分类体系（理解、生成、交互三大类）；系统梳理方法论基础（模态分词化、跨模态融合、自回归与扩散生成、大规模预训练、指令对齐、偏好优化）；整理代表性数据集、基准和评估指标，进行跨任务族的结构化比较。

Result: 提出了首个基于大型基础模型的AVI综合综述，建立了统一的分类框架，覆盖了从语音识别、声音定位到音频驱动的视频合成、视频到音频生成，再到对话、具身和智能体交互的广泛任务，并识别出同步性、空间推理、可控性和安全性等开放挑战。

Conclusion: 通过将快速扩张的AVI领域整合到统一的框架中，本文旨在为未来大规模AVI研究提供基础性参考，帮助解决当前碎片化问题并推动音频-视觉联合建模的系统性发展。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Intelligence (AVI) has emerged as a central frontier in artificial intelligence, bridging auditory and visual modalities to enable machines that can perceive, generate, and interact in the multimodal real world. In the era of large foundation models, joint modeling of audio and vision has become increasingly crucial, i.e., not only for understanding but also for controllable generation and reasoning across dynamic, temporally grounded signals. Recent advances, such as Meta MovieGen and Google Veo-3, highlight the growing industrial and academic focus on unified audio-vision architectures that learn from massive multimodal data. However, despite rapid progress, the literature remains fragmented, spanning diverse tasks, inconsistent taxonomies, and heterogeneous evaluation practices that impede systematic comparison and knowledge integration. This survey provides the first comprehensive review of AVI through the lens of large foundation models. We establish a unified taxonomy covering the broad landscape of AVI tasks, ranging from understanding (e.g., speech recognition, sound localization) to generation (e.g., audio-driven video synthesis, video-to-audio) and interaction (e.g., dialogue, embodied, or agentic interfaces). We synthesize methodological foundations, including modality tokenization, cross-modal fusion, autoregressive and diffusion-based generation, large-scale pretraining, instruction alignment, and preference optimization. Furthermore, we curate representative datasets, benchmarks, and evaluation metrics, offering a structured comparison across task families and identifying open challenges in synchronization, spatial reasoning, controllability, and safety. By consolidating this rapidly expanding field into a coherent framework, this survey aims to serve as a foundational reference for future research on large-scale AVI.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [62] [eOptShrinkQ: Near-Lossless KV Cache Compression Through Optimal Spectral Denoising and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02905)
*Pei-Chun Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出eOptShrinkQ，一种基于随机矩阵理论的两阶段KV缓存压缩方法：首先通过最优奇异值收缩提取低秩共享上下文，然后利用谱去噪恢复残差的各向同性性质，再使用TurboQuant进行近最优量化。实验表明在Llama-3.1-8B和Ministral-8B上，约2.2比特/条目的压缩质量优于TurboQuant的3.0比特，并在多针检索任务中匹配或超越未压缩的FP16。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer注意力头中的KV缓存存在低秩共享分量和全秩逐token残差的自然分解（符合尖峰随机矩阵模型），现有量化方法未充分利用这一结构特性，且标量量化假设各向同性分布但残差存在各向异性，导致需要额外的异常值处理和内积偏差校正。

Method: 两阶段压缩流水线：1) eOptShrink：基于随机矩阵理论的最优奇异值收缩，自动提取共享结构（由BBP相变自动选择秩）；2) TurboQuant：对满足薄壳性质且坐标离域化的残差进行逐向量标量量化，谱去噪恢复了标量量化所假设的各向同性，消除异常值处理和内积偏差校正需求。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B和Ministral-8B上，逐头MSE和内积保真度方面，eOptShrinkQ比TurboQuant节省近1比特/条目；LongBench（16任务）上，~2.2比特/条目优于TurboQuant的3.0比特；多针检索任务中，2.2比特接近或超过未压缩FP16。

Conclusion: 谱去噪可作为检索密集型任务的有益正则化器，随机矩阵理论为该方法提供了自动秩选择、近零内积偏差和近最优量化失真的三重理论保证。

Abstract: We show that the key-value (KV) cache in transformer attention heads admits a natural decomposition into a low-rank \emph{shared context} component and a full-rank \emph{per-token} residual, well described by the spiked random matrix model. This observation leads to eOptShrinkQ, a two-stage compression pipeline: optimal singular value shrinkage (eOptShrink) automatically extracts the shared structure, and the residual -- which satisfies the \emph{thin shell property} with delocalized coordinates -- is quantized by TurboQuant~\citep{zandieh2025turboquant}, a recently proposed per-vector scalar quantizer with near-optimal distortion guarantees. By restoring the isotropy that scalar quantization assumes, spectral denoising eliminates the need for both outlier handling and dedicated inner product bias correction, freeing those bits for improved reconstruction.
  The theoretical grounding in random matrix theory provides three guarantees: automatic rank selection via the BBP phase transition, provably near-zero inner product bias on the residual, and coordinate delocalization ensuring near-optimal quantization distortion. Experimentally, we validate eOptShrinkQ on Llama-3.1-8B and Ministral-8B across three levels: per-head MSE and inner product fidelity, where eOptShrinkQ saves nearly one bit per entry over TurboQuant at equivalent quality; end-to-end on LongBench (16 tasks), where eOptShrinkQ at $\sim$2.2 bits per entry outperforms TurboQuant at 3.0 bits; and multi-needle retrieval, where eOptShrinkQ at 2.2 bits closely matches or exceeds uncompressed FP16, suggesting that spectral denoising can act as a beneficial regularizer for retrieval-intensive tasks.

</details>


### [63] [An End-to-End Framework for Building Large Language Models for Software Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02906)
*Jingkai He,Pengfei Chen,Chenghui Wu,Shuang Liang,Ye Li,Gou Tan,Xiadao Wen,Chuanfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OpsLLM，一个面向软件运维领域的专用大语言模型，通过人工循环机制、监督微调和领域过程奖励模型，在问答和根因分析任务上显著优于现有开源和闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究因数据质量低、知识碎片化和学习不充分，未能实现高效端到端的智能运维，需要探索LLM在软件运维领域的潜力。

Method: 1) 引入Human-in-the-Loop机制从原始运维数据中筛选高质量数据构建微调数据集；2) 进行监督微调获得基础模型；3) 引入领域过程奖励模型(DPRM)在强化学习阶段优化根因分析任务的准确性和可靠性。

Result: 在问答任务上提升0.2%~5.7%，在根因分析任务上提升2.7%~70.3%，且表现出强迁移性。将开源7B、14B和32B三个版本及15K微调数据集。

Conclusion: OpsLLM有效学习和对齐了运维领域知识，在准确率上全面超越现有开源和闭源LLM，验证了所提构建流程的有效性。

Abstract: In the field of software operations, Large Language Models (LLMs) have attracted increasing attention. However, existing research has not yet achieved efficient and effective end-to-end intelligent operations due to low-quality data, fragmented knowledge and insufficient learning. To explore the potential of LLMs in software operations, we propose OpsLLM, a domain-specific LLM that supports both knowledge-based question answering (QA) and root cause analysis (RCA). Moreover, we disclose the detailed workflow for building LLMs specifically in the software operations domain. First, a Human-in-the-Loop mechanism is introduced to curate highquality data from a large collection of operational raw data and construct a fine-tuning dataset. Then, based on the data, supervised fine-tuning is conducted to achieve a base model. Furthermore, we introduce a domain process reward model (DPRM) during the reinforcement learning stage to optimize the accuracy and reliability of the fine-tuned model on RCA tasks. Experimental results on the tasks with diverse difficulties demonstrate that OpsLLMs effectively learns and aligns with the operational domain knowledge infused, outperforming existing open-source and closed-source LLMs in accuracy with improvements of 0.2%~5.7% on QA tasks and 2.7% ~70.3% on RCA tasks, while exhibiting strong transferability. Moreover, we will open-source three versions of OpsLLM with 7B, 14B and 32B parameters, along with a 15K fine-tuning dataset.

</details>


### [64] [On the Invariants of Softmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02907)
*Wonsuk Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现softmax注意力中的能量场（行中心化注意力logit）存在两类不变性：机制级不变性（代数结构导致的零和约束、秩界、谱特征）和模型级不变性（能量场方差去局域化，源于键矩阵的非相干性）。这些不变性具有实际意义，如秩界将能量场限制在低维子空间，键非相干性可作为训练监控指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索softmax注意力机制中查询-键交互的潜在结构，揭示其内在不变性质。

Method: 定义能量场（行中心化注意力logit），从机制级和模型级两个层面分析其不变性，并在多种架构的自回归语言模型上进行验证。

Result: 发现机制级不变性（零和约束、秩界、谱特征）和模型级不变性（能量场方差去局域化），后者源于键矩阵非相干性。

Conclusion: 这些不变性具有实际应用价值：秩界限制能量场为低维子空间，键非相干性可作为逐头训练监控指标。

Abstract: Softmax attention maps every query--key interaction into a probability distribution, but the underlying structure remains largely unexplored. We define the \emph{energy field}, the row-centered attention logit, and show that it exhibits invariant properties across models, architectures, and inputs.
  Two classes of invariants emerge. \emph{Mechanism-level} invariants follow from the algebraic structure of softmax attention. They include a per-row zero-sum constraint, a rank bound determined by the head dimension, and spectral signatures that follow from them. \emph{Model-level} regularities are not required by the mechanism, yet hold in every autoregressive language model we test, spanning several architecture families. The energy field distributes its variance over key positions without concentrating at a few. This delocalization traces to a property of the key matrix we call \emph{key incoherence}.
  These invariants have practical consequences. The rank bound confines the energy field to a low-dimensional subspace. Key incoherence yields a per-head training monitor. All results are verified at multiple context lengths and input texts.

</details>


### [65] [Delay, Plateau, or Collapse: Evaluating the Impact of Systematic Verification Error on RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02909)
*Kazuki Egashira,Mark Vero,Jasper Dekoninck,Florian E. Dorner,Robin Staab,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）中验证器系统性错误的影响，发现系统性的假阳性错误会导致模型性能下降甚至崩溃，而假阴性影响类似于随机噪声；错误的具体模式比整体错误率更关键，挑战了先前认为验证错误仅会减慢训练的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究认为验证器错误（如随机独立噪声）仅会减慢训练速度，对最终性能影响有限；但实际验证器（如静态代码检查器）往往存在系统性错误，可能导致模型学习到不良的持续性行为。本文旨在研究系统性验证错误对RLVR的影响。

Method: 通过在算术任务上进行受控实验，分别引入系统性的假阳性错误和假阴性错误，观察其对RLVR训练过程和最终性能的影响。

Result: 系统性假阴性错误的影响与随机噪声类似；系统性假阳性错误则会导致从次优平台到性能崩溃的多种不良行为；这些结果并非由整体错误率决定，而是由错误的具体模式决定。

Conclusion: 现实中的验证器错误会严重影响RLVR的结果，验证器质量需要超越样本级错误率来理解；先前的结论（错误仅减慢训练）在系统性错误面前不成立。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a powerful approach for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). While RLVR is designed for tasks with verifiable ground-truth answers, real-world verifiers (e.g., static code checkers) can introduce errors into the reward signal. Prior analyses have largely treated such errors as random and independent across samples, concluding that errors merely slow training with limited effect on final performance. However, practical verifiers tend to exhibit systematic errors. This introduces a risk of models learning unwanted consistent behavior from a structurally incorrect reward signal. In this work, we study the impact of such systematic verification errors on RLVR. Through controlled experiments on arithmetic tasks, we show that systematic false negatives lead to similar effects as random noise. On the other hand, systematic false positives can cause a wide range of behaviors from sub-optimal plateaus to performance collapse. Crucially, these outcomes are not determined by the overall error rate but by the specific pattern of introduced errors, making pre-hoc mitigation difficult. Our results show that, in contrast to prior conclusions, realistic verification errors can critically shape RLVR outcomes and that verifier quality has to be understood beyond its sample-level error rate.

</details>


### [66] [Generate, Filter, Control, Replay: A Comprehensive Survey of Rollout Strategies for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02913)
*Rohan Surana,Gagan Mundada,Xunyi Jiang,Chuhan Wang,Zhenwei Tang,Difan Jiao,Zihan Huang,Yuxin Xiong,Junda Wu,Sheldon Yu,Xintong Li,Raghav Jain,Nikki Kuang,Sizhe Zhou,Bowen Jin,Zhendong Chu,Tong Yu,Ryan Rossi,Kuan-Hao Huang,Jingbo Shang,Jiawei Han,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对基于强化学习的推理型大语言模型后训练中的rollout策略进行了系统综述，提出GFCR生命周期分类法（生成-过滤-控制-重放），将rollout流水线分为四个模块化阶段，并辅以可靠性、覆盖率和成本敏感性三个维度组成的标准分类法，用于刻画rollout的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已成为提升大语言模型推理能力的关键后训练工具，但rollout设计（即从prompt到终止的轨迹采样过程，包括中间推理步骤及工具/环境交互）常被低估且缺乏系统报告，亟需一个统一的框架来形式化和分析各类rollout策略。

Method: 提出GFCR（Generate-Filter-Control-Replay）生命周期分类法，将rollout流水线分解为四个模块化阶段：Generate（候选轨迹与拓扑生成）、Filter（通过验证器、裁判、批评者构建中间信号）、Control（在预算约束下进行继续/分支/停止决策）、Replay（保留跨rollout的中间产物用于自进化课程生成），并引入可靠性、覆盖率、成本敏感性三维标准分类法。

Result: 基于该框架，综合了可验证奖励RL、过程监督、裁判门控、引导式与树/片段rollout、自适应计算分配、早退与部分rollout、吞吐优化、重放/重组自改进等方法，并通过数学、代码/SQL、多模态推理、工具使用智能体及智能体技能基准等案例进行了验证。

Conclusion: 提供了将常见rollout病理映射到GFCR模块与缓解杠杆的诊断索引，指出了构建可复现、计算高效且可信的rollout流水线的开放挑战。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central post-training tool for improving the reasoning abilities of large language models (LLMs). In these systems, the rollout, the trajectory sampled from a prompt to termination, including intermediate reasoning steps and optional tool or environment interactions, determines the data the optimizer learns from, yet rollout design is often underreported. This survey provides an optimizer-agnostic view of rollout strategies for RL-based post-training of reasoning LLMs. We formalize rollout pipelines with unified notation and introduce Generate-Filter-Control-Replay (GFCR), a lifecycle taxonomy that decomposes rollout pipelines into four modular stages: Generate proposes candidate trajectories and topologies; Filter constructs intermediate signals via verifiers, judges, critics; Control allocates compute and makes continuation/branching/stopping decisions under budgets; and Replay retains and reuses artifacts across rollouts without weight updates, including self-evolving curricula that autonomously generate new training tasks. We complement GFCR with a criterion taxonomy of reliability, coverage, and cost sensitivity that characterizes rollout trade-offs. Using this framework, we synthesize methods spanning RL with verifiable rewards, process supervision, judge-based gating, guided and tree/segment rollouts, adaptive compute allocation, early-exit and partial rollouts, throughput optimization, and replay/recomposition for self-improvement. We ground the framework with case studies in math, code/SQL, multimodal reasoning, tool-using agents, and agentic skill benchmarks that evaluate skill induction, reuse, and cross-task transfer. Finally, we provide a diagnostic index that maps common rollout pathologies to GFCR modules and mitigation levers, alongside open challenges for building reproducible, compute-efficient, and trustworthy rollout pipelines.

</details>


### [67] [When Safety Geometry Collapses: Fine-Tuning Vulnerabilities in Agentic Guard Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02914)
*Ismail Hossain,Sai Puppala,Jannatul Ferdaus,Md Jahangir Alam,Yoonpyo Lee,Syed Bahauddin Alam,Sajedul Talukder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，在完全良性数据上微调的防护模型（LlamaGuard、WildGuard、Granite Guardian）会因领域专业化而完全丧失安全对齐，其根本原因是潜在安全几何结构（有害-良性表征边界）的破坏。作者提出Fisher加权安全子空间正则化（FW-SSR）方法，通过曲率感知方向权重和自适应惩罚系数，有效恢复了安全性能（Granite Guardian拒绝率从0%恢复至75%，WildGuard攻击成功率降至3.6%）。研究表明，表征几何指标（CKA、Fisher评分）比绝对位移指标更能可靠预测安全行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注通用大语言模型在微调中的安全退化，但尚未系统研究专门用于智能体管道的防护模型（guard model）在良性领域微调下的安全失效机制及其根本原因。

Method: 使用SVD提取逐层安全子空间，通过类条件激活差异追踪安全边界演化；提出FW-SSR正则化方法，结合Fisher信息矩阵的对角曲率权重与自适应λ_t（根据任务-安全梯度冲突缩放），在微调过程中保护安全表征几何结构。

Result: Granite Guardian在微调后拒绝率从85%降至0%，CKA归零，100%输出模糊；FW-SSR恢复75%拒绝率（CKA=0.983），WildGuard攻击成功率降至3.6%（低于未修改基线）。

Conclusion: 防护模型的安全表征具有高效但灾难性脆弱的特性（专业化假说），表征几何指标（CKA、Fisher评分）是比绝对位移更可靠的安全行为预测器，应在智能体部署中纳入基于几何的监控机制。

Abstract: A guard model fine-tuned on entirely benign data can lose all safety alignment -- not through adversarial manipulation, but through standard domain specialization. We demonstrate this failure across three purpose-built safety classifiers -- LlamaGuard, WildGuard, and Granite Guardian -- deployed as protection layers in agentic AI pipelines, and show that it originates in the destruction of latent safety geometry: the structured harmful -- benign representational boundary that guides classification. We extract per-layer safety subspaces via SVD on class-conditional activation differences and track how this boundary evolves under benign fine-tuning. Granite Guardian undergoes complete collapse --  refusal rate drops from 85\% to 0\%, CKA falls to zero, and 100\% of outputs become ambiguous -- a severity exceeding prior findings on general-purpose LLMs, explained by the specialization hypothesis: concentrated safety representations are efficient but catastrophically brittle. To mitigate this, we propose Fisher-Weighted Safety Subspace Regularization (FW-SSR), a training-time penalty combining (i) curvature-aware direction weights derived from diagonal Fisher information and (ii) an adaptive $λ_t$ that scales with task-safety gradient conflict. FW-SSR recovers 75\% refusal on Granite Guardian (CKA = 0.983) and reduces WildGuard's Attack Success Rate to 3.6\% -- below the unmodified baseline -- by actively sharpening the safety subspace rather than merely anchoring it. Across all three models, structural representational geometry (CKA, Fisher score) predicts safety behavior more reliably than absolute displacement metrics, establishing geometry-based monitoring as a necessary component of guard model evaluation in agentic deployments.

</details>


### [68] [From Synthesis to Clinical Assistance: A Strategy-Aware Agent Framework for Autism Intervention based on Real Clinical Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02916)
*Junhong Lai,Shuzhong Lai,Yanhao Yu,Wanlin Chen,Chenyu Yan,Haifeng Li,Lin Yao,Yueming Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ASDAgent框架，通过DoctorAgent的O-T-A-C推理循环和ChildAgent的概率行为建模，解决了AI辅助自闭症干预中数据稀缺、策略不一致和数据同质性问题，生成的对话与人类治疗师的策略分布高度接近（KL散度0.083），并将专业知识有效蒸馏至小语言模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助早期密集行为干预（EIBI）受限于数据稀缺；通用大语言模型（LLMs）难以严格遵循应用行为分析（ABA）的标准化流程，导致交互语言流畅但策略不一致。

Method: 提出ASDAgent框架，包含：(i) DoctorAgent，采用观察-思考-行动-纠正（O-T-A-C）推理循环，显式可控地执行ABA策略；(ii) ChildAgent，利用概率行为建模模拟多样化的ASD反应模式，缓解数据同质性问题。

Result: 生成对话与人类治疗师的策略分布高度接近（KL散度0.083）；在真实自闭症干预中达到近80%的策略一致性；合成数据可有效将专业知识蒸馏至小语言模型，显著提升其治疗能力。

Conclusion: ASDAgent通过显式策略建模和多样化行为模拟，有效解决了LLM在ABA干预中的策略崩溃和数据稀缺问题，为AI辅助自闭症干预提供了可行的数据生成与决策支持方案。

Abstract: The development of AI-assisted Early Intensive Behavioral Intervention (EIBI) for Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) is severely constrained by data scarcity. Furthermore, while Applied Behavior Analysis (ABA) serves as the gold standard for clinical intervention, general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to strictly adhere to its standardized procedures, often resulting in interactions that are linguistically fluent but strategically inconsistent. To address these challenges, we introduce \textsc{ASDAgent}, a strategy-aware framework designed to unify high-fidelity intervention dialogue synthesis and clinical decision support. \textsc{ASDAgent} incorporates two specialized components to solve distinct problems: (i) a \textsc{DoctorAgent} equipped with an Observe-Think-Act-Correct (O-T-A-C) reasoning loop, which resolves the issue of strategy collapse in LLMs by making ABA execution explicit and controllable; and (ii) a \textsc{ChildAgent} that utilizes probabilistic behavior modeling to mitigate data homogeneity, simulating diverse and non-deterministic ASD response patterns. Experiments demonstrate that dialogues generated by \textsc{ASDAgent} closely mirror the strategy distribution of human therapists (KL divergence: 0.083). In real autism intervention, \textsc{ASDAgent} achieves nearly 80\% strategic consistency with human experts. Moreover, we show that synthetic data produced by \textsc{ASDAgent} effectively distills professional clinical knowledge into small language models (SLMs), significantly enhancing their therapeutic capabilities.

</details>


### [69] [Mitigating the reconstruction-detection trade-off in VAE-based unsupervised anomaly detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02918)
*Agathe Senellart,Maëlys Solal,Stéphanie Allassonnière,Ninon Burgos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了β-VAE模型中重构质量与异常检测性能之间存在权衡：约束潜在空间的模型检测指标更高但重构质量更低。分析了随机种子对性能的影响，并验证了beta-scheduling和Sparse VAE两种缓解该权衡的方法，其中Sparse VAE在保持高重构质量的同时显著提升了检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分自编码器广泛用于无监督异常检测，但模型选择仍是一个开放问题：为保持完全无监督，超参数通常通过最小化正常样本的重构误差来选择。本文旨在揭示该做法存在的根本性权衡问题，并探索缓解方案。

Method: 1) 系统评估β-VAE模型中β值对重构质量和异常检测性能的影响，揭示二者之间的权衡关系。2) 分析不同随机种子下性能变异性，并将其与正常和异常潜在分布之间的距离相关联。3) 提出并验证两种缓解重构-检测权衡的方法：beta-scheduling（动态调整beta值）和Sparse VAE（稀疏变分自编码器）。

Result: β-VAE中潜在空间约束越强（β越大），异常检测指标越高，但重构质量下降；随机种子导致的性能变异与正常/异常潜在分布间距有关；Sparse VAE在保持高重构质量的同时显著提升了异常检测性能。

Conclusion: 无监督异常检测中不能单纯以重构误差作为模型选择标准，需考虑重构与检测的权衡；Sparse VAE是缓解该权衡的有效方案，而beta-scheduling也可作为替代策略。

Abstract: Variational autoencoders are widely used for unsupervised anomaly detection. Model selection however remains an open-question: to remain fully unsupervised, hyperparameters are often chosen to minimize the reconstruction error on normal samples. In this paper, we reveal a trade-off between reconstruction quality and anomaly detection among $β$-VAE models. Models with constrained latent space reach higher detection metrics but lower reconstruction quality. We also assess the performance variability across random seeds and show it is linked to the distance between normal and abnormal latent distributions. From this analysis, we justify and investigate two methods to mitigate the reconstructiondetection tradeoff: beta-scheduling and the Sparse VAE. The latter especially shows an improvement in detection while maintaining high reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [70] [DeRelayL: Sustainable Decentralized Relay Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02935)
*Haihan Duan,Tengfei Ma,Yuyang Qin,Runhao Zeng,Wei Cai,Victor C. M. Leung,Xiping Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出去中心化中继学习（DeRelayL），一种允许无许可用户以中继方式协作训练和共享模型的新型学习范式，旨在解决大模型训练门槛高、现有协作方法缺乏可持续性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型训练需要高昂的算力和资金，仅有少数科技巨头有能力承担，而作为数据生产者的普通用户（如移动用户）被排除在受益范围之外。现有的大模型访问方法要么限制用户所有权，要么缺乏可持续性，亟需一种允许普通用户协作训练和共享模型的方法。

Method: 提出去中心化中继学习（DeRelayL）范式，设计其架构和工作流程，参与者以无许可方式像中继一样贡献训练并共享模型；设计激励机制确保系统可持续性；进行理论分析和数值模拟验证有效性。

Result: 通过理论分析和数值模拟证明了DeRelayL的有效性。

Conclusion: DeRelayL作为一种新的去中心化协作学习范式，能够使普通用户以可持续的方式参与大模型训练并共享模型收益，弥合大模型训练中的资源鸿沟。

Abstract: In the era of big data, large-scale machine learning models have revolutionized various fields, driving significant advancements. However, large-scale model training demands high financial and computational resources, which are only affordable by a few technological giants and well-funded institutions. In this case, common users like mobile users, the real creators of valuable data, are often excluded from fully benefiting due to the barriers, while the current methods for accessing large-scale models either limit user ownership or lack sustainability. This growing gap highlights the urgent need for a collaborative model training approach, allowing common users to train and share models. However, existing collaborative model training paradigms, especially federated learning (FL), primarily focus on data privacy and group-based model aggregation. To this end, this paper intends to address this issue by proposing a novel training paradigm named decentralized relay learning (DeRelayL), a sustainable learning system where permissionless participants can contribute to model training in a relay-like manner and share the model. In detail, this paper presents the architecture and workflow of DeRelayL, designs incentive mechanisms to ensure sustainability, and conducts theoretical analysis and numerical simulations to demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [71] [Proteo-R1: Reasoning Foundation Models for De Novo Protein Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02937)
*Fang Wu,Weihao Xuan,Heli Qi,Hanqun Cao,Heng-Jui Chang,Zeqi Zhou,Haokai Zhao,Ma Jian,Carl Ma,Yu-Chi Cheng,Kuan Pang,Xiangru Tang,Zehong Wang,Guanlue Li,Hanchen Wang,Kejun Ying,Pan Lu,Chiho Im,Seungju Han,Peng Xia,Tinson Xu,Yinxi Li,Deyao Zhu,Pheng-Ann Heng,Naoto Yokoya,Masashi Sugiyama,Li Erran Li,Jure Leskovec,Yejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Proteo-R1 是一种推理引导的蛋白质设计框架，通过将分子理解（MLLM 识别关键功能残基）与几何生成（扩散模型条件设计）显式解耦，实现了可解释、可控且模块化的蛋白质从头设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习蛋白质设计模型缺乏 deliberative reasoning（审慎推理），直接合成分子几何结构而不明确推理哪些残基或相互作用是功能必需的，导致设计决策与连续采样动力学纠缠，限制了可解释性、可控性和生化知识的系统复用。

Method: 采用双专家架构：(1) 理解专家——多模态大语言模型（MLLM）分析蛋白质序列、结构和文本上下文，识别关键功能残基；(2) 生成专家——基于扩散的几何生成模型，以理解专家输出的残基级决策作为硬约束进行条件共设计。

Result: 实现了稳定、可解释且模块化的 LLM 推理与最先进几何生成模型的集成，将推理操作化为显式残基级承诺而非潜在文本引导。

Conclusion: Proteo-R1 通过解耦推理与生成，模拟人类专家的分子工程方式（先推理关键相互作用，再在这些约束下优化几何），为蛋白质设计提供了更高的可解释性和可控性。

Abstract: Deep learning in \emph{de novo} protein design has achieved atomic-level fidelity. However, existing models remain largely non-deliberative: they directly synthesize molecular geometries without explicitly reasoning about which residues or interactions are functionally essential. As a result, design decisions are entangled with continuous sampling dynamics, limiting interpretability, controllability, and systematic reuse of biochemical knowledge. We introduce \textbf{Proteo-R1}, a reasoning-guided protein design framework that explicitly decouples \emph{molecular understanding} from \emph{geometric generation}. Proteo-R1 adopts a dual-expert architecture in which a multimodal large language model (MLLM) serves as an \emph{understanding expert}, analyzing protein sequences, structures, and textual context to identify key functional residues that govern binding and specificity. These residue-level decisions are then passed as hard constraints to a separate diffusion-based \emph{generation expert}, which performs conditional co-design while respecting the fixed interaction anchors. This factorization mirrors how human experts approach molecular engineering: first, reasoning about critical interactions, then optimizing geometry subject to those constraints. By operationalizing reasoning as explicit residue-level commitments rather than latent textual guidance, Proteo-R1 achieves stable, interpretable, and modular integration of LLM reasoning with state-of-the-art geometric generative models. Code, data, and demos are available at https://smiles724.github.io/r1/.

</details>


### [72] [From Static Analysis to Audience Dissemination: A Training-Free Multimodal Controversy Detection Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02939)
*Zihan Ding,Ziyuan Yang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AuDisAgent，一个无需训练的多智能体框架，将多模态争议检测重新建模为动态传播过程，通过模拟观众群体对视频内容的讨论与评估来识别潜在争议内容，在丰富评论和稀疏评论场景下均显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCD方法将争议检测视为静态表示学习任务，直接从视频和评论中提取特征，未能捕捉不同观众群体的多样化视角和评价，忽略了内容传播过程中各群体对同一内容的不同解读方式。

Method: 提出AuDisAgent多智能体框架：(1) 三个筛选智能体（视频、评论、交互）分别从视觉、文本和跨模态视角进行初步评估；(2) 不一致时激活观众小组智能体，模拟不同背景和立场观众的内容讨论；(3) 仲裁智能体基于完整推理链做出最终判断。另提出评论引导策略，利用语义相似视频的历史评论解决冷启动问题。

Result: 在公开数据集上的大量实验表明，AuDisAgent在丰富评论和有限评论两种场景下均显著优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 将争议检测从静态表示学习重新定义为动态传播过程，通过模拟观众群体的讨论机制有效捕捉潜在争议内容，评论引导策略成功解决冷启动挑战。

Abstract: Multimodal controversy detection (MCD) identifies controversial content in videos and their associated user comments, to support risk management for social video platforms.Prior research frames MCD as a static representation learning task, where features are directly extracted from videos and their accompanying comments. However, these methods fail to capture the diverse perspectives and evaluations from different audience groups.
