Inspired by the real-world process of content dissemination among audiences, we propose AuDisAgent, a training-free multi-agent framework that reformulates MCD as a dynamic propagation process.Our framework explicitly models audience dissemination through a structured multi-agent system. First, three specialized Screening Agents (Video Agent, Comment Agent, and Interaction Agent) conduct initial assessments from visual, textual, and cross-modal perspectives, respectively. For samples where the three agents cannot reach a consensus, a Viewing Panel Agent is activated to simulate post-screening discussions among audiences with diverse backgrounds and stances. This mechanism models how different audience groups interpret and react to the same content, uncovering latent controversial content that may emerge during the dissemination process. Finally, an Arbitration Agent renders the final judgment based on the complete reasoning chain from the preceding steps.In addition, to address the "cold-start" scenario where newly released videos have few or no comments, we design a Comment Bootstrapping Strategy that leverages historical public comments from semantically similar videos as the initial comment context. Extensive experiments on a public dataset demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods in both rich-comment and limited-comment scenarios.

</details>


### [73] [PrismAgent: Illuminating Harm in Memes via a Zero-Shot Interpretable Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02940)
*Zihan Ding,Ziyuan Yang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PrismAgent提出了一种零样本、多智能体、可解释的模因有害内容检测框架，通过将任务类比为刑事案件调查流程，使用四个专门智能体（分析、调查、起诉、审判）协同工作，在不依赖大规模标注数据的情况下实现了优于现有零样本方法的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模因有害内容检测方法严重依赖大规模标注数据，导致训练成本高、泛化能力有限。亟需一种无需标注数据、可解释且高效的检测方法。

Method: PrismAgent将模因有害检测建模为刑事案件调查流程，包含四个阶段：(1) 分析智能体对模因进行善意和恶意假设下的改写，探测潜在意图；(2) 调查智能体从未标注数据集中检索证据，构建模因及其变体的上下文解释；(3) 起诉智能体将原始模因与三种解释配对，进行三次独立初步判断；(4) 审判智能体综合所有证据做出最终裁决。整个过程形成显式的多阶段推理链，保证可解释性。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，PrismAgent显著优于现有零样本检测方法。

Conclusion: PrismAgent通过多智能体协作框架，在零样本条件下有效检测有害模因，同时具备良好的可解释性，为减少对标注数据依赖的有害内容检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: The rapid spread of memes makes harmful content detection increasingly crucial, as effective identification can curb the circulation of misinformation. However, existing methods rely heavily on high-volume annotated data, which leads to substantial training costs and limited generalization. To address these challenges, we propose PrismAgent, a zero-shot, multi-agent, interpretable framework. PrismAgent conceptualizes this task as a criminal case investigation, employing four specialized agents responsible for the analysis, investigation, prosecution, and judgment stages within a structured collaborative workflow. In the first stage, the analyst agent paraphrases each meme under benevolent and malicious assumptions to probe its underlying intent. The investigator agent then retrieves supporting evidence from an unannotated dataset and constructs contextual interpretations for the meme and its variants. Next, the prosecutor agent performs three independent preliminary judgments by pairing the original meme with each of the three interpretations. Finally, the judge agent deliberates across all evidence to render a final verdict. Moreover, PrismAgent's explicit multi-stage reasoning chain makes the model inherently interpretable, as every intermediate step is explicitly explained rather than only producing a final detection result. Extensive experiments on three public datasets show that PrismAgent significantly outperforms existing zero-shot detection methods.

</details>


### [74] [Healthcare AI GYM for Medical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02943)
*Minbyul Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TT-OPD方法，通过将KL正则化细粒度到每一轮对话，解决了多轮医疗AI智能体在强化学习训练中的崩溃问题（冗长单轮化、工具使用减少），在18个基准中10个达到最优，平均提升3.9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床推理需要多轮交互，但目前缺乏统一的训练环境来支持基于强化学习的通用医疗AI智能体训练。

Method: 构建了\gym{}环境（10个临床域，3.6K+任务，135个专用工具，828K医学知识库），并提出Turn-level Truncated On-Policy Distillation (TT-OPD)，利用无梯度EMA教师提供每轮对话的密集、结果感知的KL正则化。

Result: TT-OPD在18个基准中10个达到最优，平均提升3.9个百分点，更快的早期收敛，可控的响应长度，持续的多轮工具使用。

Conclusion: 多轮智能体的RL训练存在长度爆炸和工具使用衰减的崩溃问题，TT-OPD通过逐轮密集正则化能有效提升训练稳定性和效率。

Abstract: Clinical reasoning demands multi-step interactions -- gathering patient history, ordering tests, interpreting results, and making safe treatment decisions -- yet a unified training environment provides the breadth of clinical domains and specialized tools to train generalizable medical AI agents through reinforcement learning remains elusive. We present a comprehensive empirical study of multi-turn agentic RL for medical AI, built on \gym{}, a gymnasium-compatible environment spanning 10 clinical domains with 3.6K+ tasks, 135 domain-specific tools, and a knowledge base of 828K medical passages. Our analysis reveals that agentic multi-turn structure degrades into verbose single-turn monologues, characterized by monotonic length explosion and a simultaneous erosion of tool-use frequency. We characterize how this collapse, alongside distillation instability, stems from the misalignment of sparse terminal rewards with sequential clinical trajectories. We find that vanilla GRPO achieves strong final accuracy on some benchmarks but suffers from training instability, evidenced by significant oscillations in response length and prolonged convergence periods. To improve training efficiency and stability, we propose Turn-level Truncated On-Policy Distillation (TT-OPD), a self-distillation framework where a gradient-free EMA teacher leverages outcome-privileged information to provide dense, outcome-aware KL regularization at every conversation turn. TT-OPD achieves the best performance on 10 of 18 benchmarks with an average +3.9~pp improvement over the non-RL baseline with faster early convergence, controlled response length, and sustained multi-turn tool use.

</details>


### [75] [RouteHijack: Routing-Aware Attack on Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02946)
*Zhiyuan Xu,Joseph Gardiner,Sana Belguith,Lichao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RouteHijack，一种针对MoE大语言模型的路由感知越狱攻击方法。通过响应驱动的专家定位识别安全关键专家和有害专家，构建路由感知的对抗后缀以抑制安全专家、促进有害专家并阻止早期拒绝。在7个MoE LLM上取得69.3%平均攻击成功率，是现有优化攻击的3.2倍，并能零样本迁移到5个MoE变体和3个MoE视觉语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对大语言模型的对抗攻击在MoE架构上存在局限性：基于提示的越狱依赖启发式搜索且迁移性差；模型干预方法需要特权访问内部表示；基于优化的输入攻击以输出为中心，且由于MoE路由机制不可微而从根本上受限。因此需要针对MoE架构特点的越狱攻击方法。

Method: RouteHijack包含两个阶段：(1) 响应驱动的专家定位：通过对比安全拒绝和有害补全下的激活值，识别安全关键专家和有害专家；(2) 路由感知对抗后缀构建：设计路由感知目标函数，同时抑制安全专家激活、促进有害专家激活，并防止生成早期阶段的拒绝行为。推理时将优化后的后缀附加到恶意提示后，仅需输入级访问。

Result: 在7个MoE LLM上平均攻击成功率达69.3%，优于现有优化攻击3.2倍；零样本迁移到5个MoE变体，平均ASR从27.7%提升至61.2%；泛化到3个MoE视觉语言模型，平均ASR从2.47%提升至38.7%。

Conclusion: 研究表明稀疏专家架构存在根本性安全漏洞，安全行为集中在少量专家上，通过路由操控即可绕过安全对齐，凸显了超越输出级对齐的防御需求。

Abstract: Safety alignment is critical for the responsible deployment of large language models (LLMs). As Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures are increasingly adopted to scale model capacity, understanding their safety robustness becomes essential. Existing adversarial attacks, however, have notable limitations. Prompt-based jailbreaks rely on heuristic search and transfer poorly, model intervention methods require privileged access to internal representations, and optimization-based input attacks remain output-centric and are fundamentally limited to MoE models due to the non-differentiable routing mechanism.
  In this paper, we present RouteHijack, a routing-aware jailbreak for MoE LLMs. Our key insight is that safety behavior is concentrated in a small subset of experts, creating an opportunity to steer model behavior by influencing routing decisions through input optimization. Building on this observation, RouteHijack first performs response-driven expert localization to identify safety-critical and harmful experts by contrasting activations under safe refusals and harmful completions. It then constructs adversarial suffixes with a routing-aware objective that suppresses safety experts, promotes harmful experts, and prevents early-stage refusal during generation. At inference time, the optimized suffix is appended to a malicious prompt, requiring only input access. Across seven MoE LLMs, RouteHijack achieves a 69.3\% average attack success rate (ASR), outperforming prior optimization-based attack by $3.2\times$. RouteHijack also transfers zero-shot across five sibling MoE variants, raising average ASR from 27.7\% to 61.2\%, and further generalizes to three MoE-based VLMs, increasing average ASR from 2.47\% to 38.7\%. These findings expose a fundamental vulnerability in sparse expert architectures and highlight the need for defenses beyond output-level alignment.

</details>


### [76] [AsymK-Talker: Real-Time and Long-Horizon Talking Head Generation via Asymmetric Kernel Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02948)
*Yuxin Lu,Qian Qiao,Jiayang Sun,Min Cao,Guibo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AsymK-Talker是一种基于扩散蒸馏的新方法，通过核条件循环生成、时间参考编码和非对称核蒸馏三个组件，解决现有语音驱动说话头生成方法在实时推理、时序一致性和长序列漂移方面的局限性，实现实时、长时序的高保真说话头生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在语音驱动说话头生成中虽提升了视觉保真度，但存在三个关键局限：(1)因果性差，无法实现实时推理；(2)与时序一致的条件不兼容；(3)长序列生成中出现渐进式漂移。这些问题阻碍了其在实时应用中的部署。

Method: AsymK-Talker包含三个核心组件：(1)核条件循环生成(KCLG)——基于因果的块式生成范式，利用运动核实现时序一致的传播；(2)时间参考编码(TRE)——将静态身份参考转换为时间感知的潜在表征以增强音视频同步；(3)非对称核蒸馏(AKD)——教师-学生蒸馏框架，教师模型以真值运动核为条件提供监督，学生模型学习从生成核中进行生成，确保长序列生成中的鲁棒性。

Result: AsymK-Talker在视觉保真度和唇形同步指标上均取得了有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: 该工作通过扩散蒸馏策略有效解决了实时性和长时序生成中的关键挑战，为实时应用中的说话头生成提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion models have markedly enhanced the visual fidelity of audio-driven talking head generation. Nevertheless, existing methods are constrained by three critical limitations: causal inefficiency that impedes real-time inference, incompatibility with temporally coherent conditioning, and progressive drift over long-horizon generation, collectively hindering their deployment in real-time applications. To overcome these challenges, we introduce AsymK-Talker, a novel diffusion-distillation method designed for real-time and long-horizon talking head generation. AsymK-Talker comprises three key components: (1) Kernel-Conditioned Loop Generation (KCLG), a causal, chunk-wise generation paradigm that leverages motion kernels to enable temporally consistent propagation; (2) Temporal Reference Encoding (TRE), which converts a static identity reference into a time-aware latent representation to enhance audio-visual synchronization; and (3) Asymmetric Kernel Distillation (AKD), a teacher-student distillation framework wherein the teacher model conditions on ground-truth motion kernels for supervision, while the student learns to generate from generated kernels, thereby ensuring robustness during extended generation sequences. AsymK-Talker achieves promising results on both visual fidelity and lip synchronization metrics.

</details>


### [77] [Disease Is a Spectral Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02949)
*John D. Mayfield,Matthew S. Rosen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于生物标志物"哈密顿量"（H = X^T X / n）的疾病转变理解方法，通过分析健康与疾病状态的协方差矩阵特征谱变化，实现对疾病轨迹的分子层面和个体层面的机制解释，并利用新患者协方差结构在疾病判别特征模态上的投影作为预后统计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有疾病转变研究方法缺乏生物标志物级别的可解释性，无法在分子层面和个体层面同时提供疾病轨迹的机制性解释，急需一种能够刻画疾病扰动对生物标志物协方差结构影响的统一框架。

Method: 定义生物标志物"哈密顿量"H = X^T X / n，其本征向量定义协调的正常模式，本征值量化模式能量；将疾病视为对健康参考哈密顿量H_0的加性扰动ΔH，导致本征值偏移与本征向量旋转；通过新患者累积协方差结构在疾病判别本征模态上的投影进行预后。

Result: 该框架能够量化疾病扰动的严重程度（本征值偏移和本征向量旋转），且新患者的协方差投影构成最优预后统计量，可提升疾病预后精度。

Conclusion: 该方法是一个通用白皮书框架，适用于从癌症到神经退行性疾病等多种疾病体系，提供具有生物标志物级别可解释性的疾病转变机制理解。

Abstract: We propose a novel method of understanding disease transformation from a healthy baseline with biomarker-level explainability. By modeling the biomarker covariance matrices of healthy controls and disease states, the perturbation can be individually characterized to accomplish mechanistic explanations of disease trajectories, both at a molecular level and for individual patients. Given a cohort of n patients each measured on p biomarkers, we define the biomarker "Hamiltonian" H = X^T X / n \in R^{p \times p}, where X \in R^{n \times p} is the covariant biomarker matrix. The eigenvectors of H define a set of normal modes of biomarker coordination, and the eigenvalues quantify the energy carried by each mode. In the healthy state, the reference Hamiltonian H_0 governs this structure where disease perturbs H_0 by an additive operator ΔH, thus shifting eigenvalues and rotating eigenvectors in proportion to the severity of pathological disruption. We formalize this framework, derive the spectral change given a disease perturbation, and demonstrate that the projection of a newly diagnosed patient's cumulative biomarker covariance structure onto disease-discriminant eigenmodes constitutes an optimal prognostic statistic for greater precision in disease prognosis. This work serves as a veritable white paper with application across a panoply of disease frameworks from cancer to neurodegenerative disorders.

</details>


### [78] [ZeRO-Prefill: Zero Redundancy Overheads in MoE Prefill Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02960)
*Zhaoyuan Su,Olatunji Ruwase,Karthik Ganesan,Aurick Qiao,Samyam Rajbhandari,Juncheng Yang,Yue Cheng,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZeRO-Prefill 是一个专为 MoE 模型预填充（prefill-only）场景设计的服务系统，通过异步专家权重聚合（AsyncEP）替代传统的激活路由 AllToAll，将通信与计算重叠，从而消除分布式并行策略中的冗余开销，在 Qwen3-235B-A22B 上实现 1.35-1.59x 的吞吐提升，GPU 利用率达 29.8-36.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MoE 模型的并行策略（张量/专家/流水线并行）源于解码时代的设计，将专家放置与同步激活路由耦合，导致大量冗余计算、通信和同步开销，严重限制了预填充（prefill-only）工作负载的服务效率。预填充任务瓶颈不在于计算，而在于分布式执行带来的内存压力与通信开销。

Method: 提出 ZeRO-Prefill 系统，后端采用异步专家并行（AsyncEP），利用大批次预填充逐层前向传播的计算密集特性，将传统的逐层激活 AllToAll 替换为异步权重 AllGather，使其完全与计算重叠。前端通过前缀感知路由和真实 FLOPs 负载追踪，联合实施物理推导的饱和阈值。

Result: 在 Qwen3-235B-A22B 上，覆盖四种硬件/精度配置，ZeRO-Prefill 在真实工作负载上比最强分布式基线提升 1.35-1.37x 吞吐，在长上下文合成负载上最高提升 1.59x，每 GPU 模型 FLOPs 利用率维持在 29.8-36.2%。

Conclusion: 解耦专家放置与同步激活路由，用异步权重聚合替代激活路由通信，可显著提升 MoE 模型预填充服务效率，证明了预填充场景下应以权重为中心而非以激活为中心进行专家分发。

Abstract: Production LLM workloads increasingly serve discriminative tasks, such as classification, recommendation, and verification, whose answers are read from the logits of a single prefill pass with no autoregressive decoding. Serving these prefill-only workloads on mixture-of-experts (MoE) models is bottlenecked not by compute but by the distributed execution required to fit the model: existing parallel strategies (tensor, expert, and pipeline parallelism) trade memory pressure for redundant computation, communication, and synchronization, severely degrading MoE prefill serving efficiency. We observe that these overheads stem from coupling expert placement with synchronous activation routing -- a design inherited from the decoding era. The long, compute-bound forward passes of large-batch prefill open a per-layer window wide enough to stream expert weights in the background, replacing per-layer activation AllToAll with asynchronous weight AllGather fully overlapped with computation. We propose ZeRO-Prefill, a prefill-only serving system whose backend, AsyncEP (Asynchronous Expert Parallelism), gathers experts by weight rather than routing them by activation, and whose frontend co-enforces a physically-derived saturation threshold through prefix-aware routing and true-FLOPs load tracking. On Qwen3-235B-A22B across four hardware/precision configurations, ZeRO-Prefill delivers 1.35-1.37x throughput over the strongest distributed baseline on real-world workloads and up to 1.59x on long-context synthetic workloads, sustaining 29.8-36.2% per-GPU model FLOPs utilization.

</details>


### [79] [ISAAC: Auditing Causal Reasoning in Deep Models for Drug-Target Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02962)
*Barbara Tarantino,Sun Kim,Yijingxiu Lu,Paolo Giudici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ISAAC是一个事后框架，通过对冻结的DTI预测模型进行匹配的机制性和虚假输入级干预来评估先验相对结构灵敏度，揭示了AUROC相近（约3%差异）的模型在推理分数上存在约25%的相对差异，这些差异是传统准确率指标无法检测的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习DTI预测模型即使不依赖有机制意义的分子特征也能取得良好基准性能，而基于准确率的评估无法检测这一局限，因此需要一种不依赖预测准确率的、评估模型推理结构合理性的方法。

Method: ISAAC框架通过对冻结模型施加匹配的机制性干预（mechanistic intervention）和虚假干预（spurious intervention），对比模型对两类干预的结构灵敏度，从而量化模型的先验相对结构敏感性，独立于预测准确性。

Result: 在Davis基准上对三个基于序列的DTI架构应用ISAAC，发现AUROC相近（约3%差异）的模型之间推理分数存在约25%的相对差异，且在不同训练种子、干预种子和两种不同的扰动算子下结果稳定。

Conclusion: 标准准确率指标无法检测模型在推理结构上的差异，事后结构审计应作为分子建模科学机器学习中标准性能评估的补充。

Abstract: Deep learning models for drug--target interaction (DTI) prediction often achieve strong benchmark performance without necessarily relying on mechanistically meaningful molecular features, a limitation that standard accuracy-based evaluation cannot detect. We introduce ISAAC (Intervention-based Structural Auditing Approach for Causal Reasoning), a post-hoc framework that evaluates prior-relative structural sensitivity by probing frozen models through matched mechanistic and spurious input-level interventions, independently of predictive accuracy. Applied to three sequence-based DTI architectures on the Davis benchmark, ISAAC reveals approximately 25\% relative differences in reasoning scores across models with comparable AUROC (within around 3\%), stable across training and intervention seeds and two distinct perturbation operators. These discrepancies, undetectable under conventional accuracy metrics, motivate the use of post-hoc structural auditing as a complement to standard performance evaluation in scientific machine learning for molecular modeling.

</details>


### [80] [Joint Energy Management and Coordinated AIGC Workload Scheduling for Distributed Data Centers: A Diffusion-Aided Reward Shaping Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02965)
*Yang Fu,Peng Qin,Liming Chen,Zihao Zhang,Hao Yu,Yifei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种联合能量管理与协调式AIGC工作负载调度框架，通过显式服务质量建模、扩散模型辅助的奖励塑形与深度强化学习相结合，在应对模型异质性、稀疏奖励等挑战的同时，实现了数据中心能耗成本与服务质量之间的平衡优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIGC服务在云数据中心产生的计算负载快速增长，迫切需要策略性调度以降低能耗成本并保证内容生成质量。然而AIGC服务存在三大挑战：跨服务提供商的模型异质性、服务质量的隐式评估困难、推理过程的复杂控制。

Method: 提出联合能量管理与协调式AIGC工作负载调度框架：1) 引入服务质量的显式数学表征，支持任务在ASP间转移和细粒度推理过程配置；2) 联合考虑数据中心多种能源资源以提升用电灵活性；3) 建立系统效用最大化问题；4) 针对调度决策耦合导致的奖励稀疏问题，提出扩散模型辅助的奖励塑形方法，通过多步去噪过程合成互补奖励信号，并与DRL无缝集成。

Result: 基于真实模型和数据集的实验表明，该方案有效适应了电价波动和AIGC模型异质性，在学习收敛速度和系统效用方面均优于基准方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架成功解决了AIGC工作负载调度中的模型异质性、服务质量评估和稀疏奖励等关键挑战，实现了高效节能的AIGC服务调度。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence-generated content (AIGC) has emerged as a transformative paradigm for automating the creation of diverse and customized content, giving rise to rapidly growing computational workloads in cloud data centers. It is imperative for AIGC service providers (ASPs) to strategically schedule AIGC workloads to reduce data center energy costs while guaranteeing high-quality content generation. However, the distinctive characteristics of AIGC services pose critical challenges, including model heterogeneity across ASPs, implicit service quality evaluation, and complex inference process control. To tackle these challenges, we propose a joint energy management and coordinated AIGC workload scheduling framework, which introduces an explicit mathematical characterization of service quality to promote both job transfer among ASPs and fine-grained inference process configuration. Moreover, various energy resources within data centers are jointly considered to enhance power usage flexibility. Subsequently, a system utility maximization problem is formulated to balance AIGC service revenue with operational penalties and costs. Nevertheless, the strong coupling among job scheduling decisions induces severe reward sparsity, which limits the effectiveness of existing deep reinforcement learning (DRL) algorithms. To address this issue, we develop a diffusion model-aided reward shaping approach to synthesize complementary reward signals through a multi-step denoising process. This approach is seamlessly integrated with DRL to enable efficient learning of scheduling policies under sparse environmental feedback. Experiments based on real-world models and datasets demonstrate that our scheme effectively accommodates electricity price fluctuations and AIGC model heterogeneity, while achieving superior learning convergence and system utility compared with benchmark methods.

</details>


### [81] [AutoRAGTuner: A Declarative Framework for Automatic Optimization of RAG Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02967)
*Xintan Zeng,Yongchao Liu,Yice Luo,Jiajun Zhen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoRAGTuner 是一个声明式、配置驱动的框架，通过模块化架构、领域元素模型（DEM）和自适应贝叶斯优化引擎，自动完成 RAG 系统的构建、执行、评估和优化全生命周期，在多种 RAG 流水线上均优于默认基线，并将架构调整的代码改动量减少高达 95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 系统性能对复杂架构设计和超参数配置高度敏感，但目前依赖低效的手动调优，缺乏自动化、系统化的优化方案。

Method: 1) 模块化架构与组件注册机制解耦流水线阶段；2) 提出领域元素模型（DEM），以原子元素及双向指针统一表示节点、边和超边等异构数据；3) 集成自适应贝叶斯优化引擎进行端到端超参数调优。

Result: 在从普通到图结构的多类 RAG 流水线上均优于默认基线；声明式配置语言使架构调整的代码改动量减少高达 95%。

Conclusion: AutoRAGTuner 为构建可演进、可复用的 RAG 系统提供了系统可优化的基础框架，显著降低工程开销。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances LLMs, but performance is highly sensitive to complex architecture designs and hyper-parameter configurations, which currently rely on inefficient manual tuning. We present AutoRAGTuner, a declarative, configuration-driven framework that automates the RAG life cycle: construction, execution,evaluation, and optimization. AutoRAGTuner employs a modular architecture to decouple pipeline stages through a component registration mechanism. To unify heterogeneous data, we introduce the Domain-Element Model (DEM), representing objects as atomic elements with bidirectional pointers to support nodes, edges, and hyperedges. Furthermore, AutoRAGTuner integrates an adaptive Bayesian optimization engine for end-to-end hyper-parameter tuning. Experimental results demonstrate AutoRAGTuner's architectural generality: across diverse RAG pipelines, ranging from vanilla to graph-based, the framework consistently outperforms default baselines. Notably, AutoRAGTuner significantly mitigates engineering overhead, where its declarative configuration language enables a up to 95\% reduction in code churn for architectural adjustments. Overall, AutoRAGTuner provides a systematically optimizable foundation for building evolvable and reusable RAG systems.

</details>


### [82] [Finite-Size Gradient Transport in Large Language Model Pretraining: From Cascade Size to Intensive Transport Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02968)
*Ping Wang,Yan-Qi Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于五个可观测量的有限尺寸梯度传输框架，用于分析真实语言模型训练。通过对Pico-LM和Pythia两个模型系列的分析，发现两者共享近一致的级联尺寸骨架，但处于不同的传输机制中。Pico-LM显示出正持续时间缩放和负密集效率缩放，而Pythia接近D=1基线，仅有弱正效率尺度依赖性。性能关联主要体现在v_rel和归一化级联持续时间通道，而D(t)作为共享尺寸骨架，无显著指数级性能关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型训练缺乏一种能够分离级联尺寸、持续时间、绝对传输和密集传输效率的有限尺寸梯度传输框架，无法有效区分不同模型系列在传输机制上的差异。

Method: 基于五个可观测(D,z,β,δ,v_rel)构建有限尺寸梯度传输框架，在Pico-LM四个尺度125个对齐步骤上进行直接原始梯度测量，同时在Pythia五个尺度上构建153个对齐检查点差分更新场的配套数据集。通过随机场控制实验验证对比差异源于真实偏离而非空校准差异。

Result: 两个模型系列共享近一致的级联尺寸骨架但处于不同传输机制：Pico-LM正持续时间缩放、负密集效率缩放；Pythia接近D=1基线，弱正效率尺度依赖。随机场控制在两个通道中产生几乎匹配的空底值，步态幂律可压缩性也存在差异。

Conclusion: 提出了一个可复用的传输测量框架，但并未声称存在通用固定点或基于第一性原理推导神经缩放定律。性能关联主要在通道级别，由v_rel和归一化级联持续时间承载，D(t)作为共享尺寸骨架无显著指数级性能关联。

Abstract: We introduce a finite-size gradient-transport framework for real language-model training, based on five observables $(D,z,β,δ,v_{\mathrm{rel}})$ that separate cascade size, duration, absolute transport, and intensive transport efficiency. We analyze direct raw-gradient measurements from Pico-LM across four scales and 125 aligned steps, together with a five-scale Pythia companion dataset built from 153 aligned checkpoint-difference update fields. The same algebraic closure holds in both families, and both share a near-unity cascade-size backbone, but they occupy distinct transport regimes: Pico-LM shows positive duration scaling and negative intensive-efficiency scaling, whereas Pythia remains near the $D=1$ baseline with only weak positive efficiency scale dependence. Randomized-field controls give nearly matched null floors in the intensive and duration channels, indicating that the contrast reflects different real departures from a shared null skeleton rather than different null calibrations. The families also differ in stepwise power-law compressibility: Pico-LM retains clean duration and efficiency power laws, whereas Pythia preserves the size backbone but shows weaker one-slope compressibility in those channels. External performance associations are correspondingly channel-level, carried mainly by $v_{\mathrm{rel}}$ and normalized cascade duration, while $D(t)$ acts as a shared size backbone without a significant exponent-level performance association. These results support a reusable transport measurement framework without claiming a universal fixed point or a first-principles derivation of neural scaling laws.

</details>


### [83] [Multilingual Safety Alignment via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02971)
*Ruiyang Qin,Qingzhuo Wang,Dongrui Liu,Qiang Li,Zhihua Wei,Wen Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出跨语言安全迁移框架MSD，通过自蒸馏将LLM的高资源语言安全能力迁移至低资源语言，无需目标语言响应数据；并提出DPSW权重策略优化蒸馏目标，在多语言越狱攻击场景下显著提升安全性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在多语言场景下存在严重的安全不对齐问题：高资源语言有强安全防护，但低资源语言易受越狱攻击。现有安全对齐方法依赖每目标语言的高质量响应数据，成本高昂且难以生成。

Method: 提出MSD框架，通过自蒸馏将LLM本身在高资源语言（如英语）的安全能力迁移至低资源语言（如爪哇语），仅需多语言查询数据。实现on-policy和off-policy两种MSD方法。提出DPSW（双视角安全加权），从教师和学生两个视角联合计算散度度量，自适应增加安全关键令牌的惩罚权重，降低非关键令牌权重。

Result: 在多种代表性LLM及多语言越狱和效用基准上，该方法一致获得优越的多语言安全性能；能有效泛化至更具挑战性的数据集和未见过的语言，同时保持模型通用能力。

Conclusion: 跨语言自蒸馏是一种低成本、高效的多语言安全对齐方案，DPSW进一步优化了安全关键信息的迁移，为多语言LLM安全部署提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit severe multilingual safety misalignment: they possess strong safeguards in high-resource languages but remain highly vulnerable to jailbreak attacks in low-resource languages. Current safety alignment methods generally rely on high-quality response data for each target language, which is expensive and difficult to generate. In this paper, we propose a cross-lingual safeguard transfer framework named Multilingual Self-Distillation (MSD). This framework transfers an LLM's inherent safety capabilities from high-resource (e.g., English) to low-resource (e.g., Javanese) languages, overcoming the need for response data in any language. Our framework is flexible and can be integrated with different self-distillation strategies. Specifically, we implement two concrete methods -- on-policy MSD and off-policy MSD -- both of which enable effective cross-lingual safety transfer using only multilingual queries. Furthermore, we propose Dual-Perspective Safety Weighting (DPSW), a divergence measure to optimize the distillation objective. By jointly considering the perspectives of both the teacher and the student, DPSW adaptively increases the penalty weights on safety-critical tokens while reducing the weights on non-critical tokens. Extensive experiments on representative LLMs across diverse multilingual jailbreak and utility benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently achieves superior multilingual safety performance. Notably, it generalizes effectively to more challenging datasets and unseen languages while preserving the model's general capabilities.

</details>


### [84] [Structured Diffusion Bridges: Inductive Bias for Denoising Diffusion Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02973)
*Eitan Kosman,Gabriele Serussi,Chaim Basking*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种扩散桥框架，通过对齐约束而非完全依赖配对数据来限制模态翻译的解空间，在无配对、半配对和全配对场景下均表现一致，且在配对要求大幅放宽时仍能达到接近全配对的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模态翻译任务存在固有的欠约束问题（多种跨模态映射可能产生相同的边缘分布），而现有方法大多依赖完全配对数据集，施加了单一的数据驱动约束，限制了模型的泛化能力。

Method: 提出一种扩散桥框架，通过对齐约束来刻画并限制可采纳解空间，将配对监督视为可选的启发式信息而非必要条件，从而支持无配对、半配对和全配对等多种监督水平。

Result: 在合成数据和真实模态翻译基准上，该方法在无配对、半配对和全配对三种设置下均表现一致；在配对要求大幅放宽时仍能达到接近全配对的质量，且在完全无配对场景下依然适用。

Conclusion: 扩散桥可以作为超越全配对数据的模态翻译的灵活基础框架，显著降低了对配对数据的依赖。

Abstract: Modality translation is inherently under-constrained, as multiple cross-modal mappings may yield the same marginals. Recent work has shown that diffusion bridges are effective for this task. However, most existing approaches rely on fully paired datasets, thereby imposing a single data-driven constraint. We propose a diffusion-bridge framework that characterizes the space of admissible solutions and restricts it via alignment constraints, treating paired supervision as an optional heuristic rather than a prerequisite. We validate our method on synthetic and real modality translation benchmarks across unpaired, semi-paired, and paired regimes, showing consistent performance across supervision levels. Notably, \textbf{it achieves near fully-paired quality with a substantial relaxation in pairing requirements, and remaining applicable in the unpaired regime}. These results highlight diffusion bridges as a flexible foundation for modality translation beyond fully paired data.

</details>


### [85] [Mixed-Precision Information Bottlenecks for On-Device Trait-State Disentanglement in Bipolar Agitation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03039)
*Joydeep Chandra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MP-IB框架，将混合精度量化视为信息瓶颈，利用FP16（1024比特）与INT4（128比特）的8倍信息不对称性实现双相障碍躁狂状态与说话人身份的解耦，在Bridge2AI-Voice上相关系数达0.117，零样本迁移AUC=0.817，延迟23.4ms，模型仅617KB，可在低于20美元的边缘设备上实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的边缘设备上，通过语音生物标志物连续监测双相障碍躁狂状态，需要将稳定的说话人特质与波动的情感状态分离开来，现有方法在解耦效率与计算开销方面存在局限。

Method: 提出MP-IB框架，核心创新是将混合精度量化视为信息瓶颈：FP16头部（1024比特）编码说话人身份，INT4头部（128比特）捕捉躁狂状态，实现8倍信息不对称性而无需对抗训练；并辅以动态精度调度和多尺度时序融合。

Result: Bridge2AI-Voice数据集（N=833，4次/人，严格说话人无关交叉验证）上rho=0.117（95%CI:[0.089,0.145]，p=0.003），优于WavLM-Adapter（rho=-0.042）、beta VAE（rho=0.089）和手工韵律特征（rho=0.031）2.8-15.9个绝对点；CREMA-D零样本迁移AUC=0.817；身份泄露抑制至接近随机水平（EER=0.42，MIA-AUC=0.52）；端到端延迟23.4ms，模型大小617KB。

Conclusion: MP-IB框架通过精度驱动的信息瓶颈设计，在极低资源开销下有效实现了说话人特质与临床状态的解耦，证明了混合精度量化可作为一种新的表征学习范式，适用于边缘设备上的实时精神健康监测。

Abstract: Continuous monitoring of bipolar disorder agitation via voice biomarkers requires disentangling stable speaker traits from volatile affective states on resource-constrained edge devices. We introduce MP-IB, the first framework to treat mixed-precision quantization as an information bottleneck for clinical trait-state separation. The core insight is that numerical precision itself controls capacity: an FP16 trait head (1,024 bits) encodes speaker identity, while an INT4 state head (128 bits) captures agitation, yielding 8x information asymmetry without adversarial training. We augment this with Dynamic Precision Scheduling and Multi-Scale Temporal Fusion. On Bridge2AI-Voice (N=833, 4 sessions/participant, strict speaker-independent CV), MP-IB achieves rho = 0.117 (95\% CI: [0.089, 0.145], p=0.003 vs. chance), outperforming 94M-parameter WavLM-Adapter with in-domain SSL continuation (rho = -0.042), beta VAE disentanglement (rho = 0.089), and hand-crafted prosody (rho = 0.031) by 2.8--15.9 points absolute. Zero-shot transfer to CREMA-D achieves AUC=0.817. Identity leakage is suppressed to near-random (EER=0.42, MIA-AUC=0.52). End-to-end latency is 23.4 ms with a 617 KB footprint, enabling real-time monitoring on sub 20 dollar devices.

</details>


### [86] [TCD-Arena: Assessing Robustness of Time Series Causal Discovery Methods Against Assumption Violations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03045)
*Gideon Stein,Niklas Penzel,Tristan Piater,Joachim Denzler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TCD-Arena，一个模块化、高度可定制的测试工具包，用于评估时间序列因果发现算法在逐步加剧的假设违反情况下的鲁棒性。通过约3000万次因果发现尝试的实证研究，揭示了33种不同假设违反的细微鲁棒性特征，并发现集成方法有潜力提升整体鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现框架在实际应用中因依赖强且通常不可验证的假设，以及缺乏稳健的性能评估而受限。为克服这些局限并推进实证因果发现评估，作者开发了TCD-Arena。

Method: 采用模块化、高度可定制和可扩展的设计，构建TCD-Arena测试工具包，针对时间序列因果发现算法，在逐步加剧的假设违反条件下进行鲁棒性评估，并开展了约3000万次个体因果发现尝试的大规模实证研究。

Result: 揭示了33种不同假设违反条件下的细微鲁棒性特征，并发现因果发现集成方法具有提升整体鲁棒性的潜力。

Conclusion: TCD-Arena能够促进开发适用于多样合成数据和潜在真实世界数据条件的可靠因果发现方法。

Abstract: Causal Discovery (CD) is a powerful framework for scientific inquiry. Yet, its practical adoption is hindered by a reliance on strong, often unverifiable assumptions and a lack of robust performance assessment. To address these limitations and advance empirical CD evaluation, we present TCD-Arena, a modularized, highly customizable, and extendable testing kit to assess the robustness of time series CD algorithms against stepwise more severe assumption violations. For demonstration, we conduct an extensive empirical study comprising around 30 million individual CD attempts and reveal nuanced robustness profiles for 33 distinct assumption violations. Further, we investigate CD ensembles and find that they have the potential to improve general robustness, which has implications for real-world applications. With this, we strive to ultimately facilitate the development of CD methods that are reliable for a diverse range of synthetic and potentially real-world data conditions.

</details>


### [87] [Adaptive Negative Scheduling for Graph Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03076)
*Adnan Ali,Jinlong Li,Syed Muhammad Israr,Ali Kashif Bashir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdNGCL提出了一种自适应负样本调度框架HANS，通过损失门控和预算约束将负样本选择分为困难、中等、容易三个层级，并动态调整步长，在9个基准图数据集上取得了7个最优、2个次优的SOTA性能，同时实现了计算成本的可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图对比学习方法依赖静态负采样，无法适应训练过程中负样本信息量的动态变化，且忽视了负采样的计算成本。

Method: 提出HANS调度器，将负样本选择建模为损失门控、预算约束的分层过程（困难/中等/容易三层），基于对比损失趋势在全局和每类预算下动态调整步长，并周期性刷新样本以维持多样性。

Result: 在9个基准图数据集上，AdNGCL取得7个最优、2个次优的准确率结果，同时实现了对计算成本的显式控制。

Conclusion: 预算感知、损失敏感的调度策略是提升表征学习鲁棒性和效率的有效通用策略。

Abstract: Graph contrastive learning (GCL) has become a central paradigm for self-supervised representation learning in computational intelligence, with applications spanning recommendation, anomaly detection, and personalization. A key limitation of existing methods is their reliance on static negative sampling, which fails to account for the dynamic informativeness and computational cost of negatives during training. We propose AdNGCL, an adaptive negative scheduling framework with a hardness-aware scheduler (HANS) that formulates negative selection as a loss-gated, budget-constrained process across hard, intermediate, and easy strata. The scheduler dynamically adjusts step sizes based on contrastive loss trends under both global and per-category budgets, while periodically refreshing samples to maintain diversity without exceeding compute constraints. Experiments on nine benchmark graph datasets demonstrate that AdNGCL consistently advances state-of-the-art performance, achieving the best accuracy on seven datasets and second-best on the remaining two, while offering explicit control over computational cost. These results highlight the value of budget-aware, loss-sensitive scheduling as a general strategy for improving the robustness and efficiency of representation learning in emerging computational intelligence applications.

</details>


### [88] [Adaptive Data Compression and Reconstruction for Memory-Bounded EEG Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03085)
*Chengcheng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ADaCoRe方法，通过形态感知的选择、压缩与重建，在无监督个体持续学习（UICL）中实现EEG信号的高效内存利用，在三个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UICL方法通常存储完整历史样本，违背了持续学习避免重训练的目标；同时EEG信号存在显著噪声和个体差异，在有限标注下难以实现鲁棒个性化。

Method: 提出ADaCoRe流水线，包含显著性驱动的关键帧保护、有理多相压缩、伴随重建（对保护索引进行逐字覆写）以及基于原型置信度的自适应示例维护。

Result: 在ISRUC和FACED数据集上，分别提升至少+2.7和+15.3 ACC；消融实验量化了压缩-保真度权衡，可视化验证了压缩与重建过程中关键EEG形态的保持。

Conclusion: ADaCoRe通过形态感知的压缩与选择性重建，在严格内存约束下有效解决了UICL中的EEG个性化问题，兼顾了压缩效率与关键信息保真度。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) signals provide millisecond-level temporal resolution but their analysis is limited by remarkable noise and inter-subject variability, making robust personalization difficult under limited annotations. Unsupervised Individual Continual Learning (UICL) has been proposed to address this practical challenge, where a model pretrained on a labeled cohort must adapt online to unlabeled subject streams under strict memory constraints. However, existing UICL methods typically store full past samples, which undermine the continual learning goal of avoiding retraining. Observing that EEG signals exhibit well-structured morphologies to be exploited via morphology-aware selection, compression, and reconstruction, here we propose Adaptive Data Compression and Reconstruction (ADaCoRe) for UICL. This is a memory-efficient pipeline composed of saliency-driven keyframe protection, rational polyphase compression, adjoint reconstruction with verbatim overwrite on protected indices, and prototype-confidence selection for adaptive exemplar maintenance. Across three representative benchmarks, ADaCoRe consistently outperforms recent strong baselines under tight buffer regimes (eg., the performance gains are at least +2.7 and +15.3 ACC on ISRUC and FACED datasets, respectively). Ablation studies quantify compression-fidelity trade-offs and highlight the contribution of each design, while visualizations confirm the preservation of key EEG morphology during compression and reconstruction.

</details>


### [89] [Attribution-Guided Masking for Robust Cross-Domain Sentiment Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03091)
*Shubham Harkare,Arvind Yogesh Suresh Babu,Yash Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为AGM的训练时干预方法，通过梯度归因掩码损失动态检测并惩罚虚假标记，在无目标域标签的零样本情感分类迁移中提升泛化性能，同时提供标记级可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练Transformer模型在域内情感分类上表现优异，但迁移到域外数据时性能严重下降。作者假设这种泛化差距源于模型过度依赖域特定的虚假标记，并指出现有的词级归因漂移方法无法预测该差距。

Method: 提出归因引导掩码（AGM），包含核心梯度归因掩码损失（L_mask），可选配反事实对比损失以增强域不变表示。AGM在微调过程中动态检测并惩罚高归因的域特定虚假标记，无需目标域标签或人工标注。

Result: 在四个不同域间的严格零样本迁移设置下，AGM在Sentiment140最难迁移任务上达到竞争性泛化性能（Δ=0.244），接近DANN(0.264)、DRO(0.248)、Fish(0.247)、IRM(0.238)等强基线。消融实验证实归因引导掩码是核心组件，移除或替换为随机标记选择会显著降低性能。

Conclusion: AGM能够有效抑制域特定虚假标记（如@提及、话题标签、俚语）的归因，将模型依赖转向域不变的情感标记，同时提供标记级可解释性，明确了哪些特征驱动了泛化差距。

Abstract: While pre-trained Transformer models achieve high accuracy on in-domain sentiment classification, they frequently experience severe performance degradation when transferring to out-of-domain data. We hypothesize that this generalization gap is driven by reliance on domain-specific spurious tokens. After demonstrating that post-hoc-token-level attribution drift fails to predict this gap, we propose Attribution-Guided Masking (AGM), a training time intervention that dynamically detects and penalizes highly attributed spurious tokens during fine-tuning. AGM's core component is a gradient based attribution masking loss ($\mathcal{L}_{mask}$), which can optionally be combined with a counterfactual contrastive loss to enforce domain-invariant representations, all without requiring target-domain labels or human annotation. Evaluated in a strict zero-shot transfer setting across four diverse domains with eight random seeds, AGM achieves competitive generalization compared to five strong baselines on the hardest transfer (Sentiment140): $Δ$ = 0.244 versus DANN (0.264), DRO (0.248), Fish (0.247), and IRM (0.238), while uniquely providing token-level interpretability into which features drive the generalization gap. Our qualitative analysis confirms that AGM suppresses attribution on domain-specific tokens such as @mentions, hashtags, and slang, shifting reliance toward domain-invariant sentiment markers. Our ablation study further confirms that attribution-guided masking is the critical component: removing it or replacing it with random token selection consistently degrades performance on difficult transfers.

</details>


### [90] [When Prompts Interact: Assessing Prompt Arithmetic for Deconfounding under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03096)
*Zhecheng Sheng,Yongsen Tan,Xiruo Ding,Trevor Cohen,Serguei Pakhomov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyPA (Hybrid Prompt Arithmetic) 通过将任务提示与线性化混淆变量提示相结合，在无需全参数微调的情况下，有效缓解了分类任务中模型依赖混淆变量导致的分布偏移鲁棒性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类模型中存在的捷径学习（shortcut learning）现象导致模型依赖混淆变量（spurious features），在分布偏移下性能显著下降。任务算术虽能通过减法移除不想要的信号，但需要全参数微调，计算成本高昂。提示微调提供了参数高效的替代方案，但基于提示的任务算术能否有效限制对混淆特征的依赖尚不清楚。

Method: 提出混合提示算术（HyPA），将任务提示（task prompts）与线性化混淆提示（linearized confounder prompts）通过提示算术相结合，以抵消虚假相关性。具体地，HyPA 同时学习任务相关提示和混淆变量提示，并通过算术操作（如减法）来消除混淆信号的影响。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，HyPA 在分布偏移下始终优于基于提示算术的基线方法，实现了更好的鲁棒性-性能权衡。进一步的表示分析表明，HyPA 缓解混淆效应的机制有两种可能路径：减少混淆信号对预测的影响，或在表示层面抑制混淆信号。

Conclusion: HyPA 是一种参数高效、有前景的鲁棒性提升方法，能够有效应对混淆变量导致的分布偏移问题，为基于提示的参数高效微调在鲁棒性研究中提供了新的方向。

Abstract: In classification tasks, models may rely on confounding variables to achieve strong in-distribution performance, capturing spurious features that fail under distribution shift. This shortcut behavior leads to substantial degradation in out-of-distribution settings. Task arithmetic offers a potential solution by removing unwanted signals via subtraction of secondary model updates, but it typically requires full fine-tuning, which is computationally expensive. Prompt tuning provides a parameter-efficient alternative by adapting models through a small set of trainable virtual tokens. Task arithmetic on the resulting prompts presents an appealing alternative to operations on entire models, but the extent to which this approach can limit reliance on spurious features remains to be established. In this work, we study whether composing soft prompts through task arithmetic improves robustness to confounding shifts. We propose Hybrid Prompt Arithmetic (HyPA), which combines task prompts with linearized confounder prompts to counteract spurious correlations. Across multiple benchmarks, HyPA consistently improves the robustness-performance trade-off relative to prompt-arithmetic baselines under distribution shift. We further analyze how HyPA affects hidden representations and find evidence consistent with it mitigating confounding either by reducing the influence of confounder signals on predictions or by suppressing them in the representation. These results establish HyPA as a parameter-efficient and promising approach for improving robustness under confounding shifts in the evaluated setting.

</details>


### [91] [Gated Subspace Inference for Transformer Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03109)
*Stephen J. Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需重训练、无需修改架构的Transformer推理加速方法，通过利用每层token激活流形的低有效秩，将激活向量分解为子空间分量和残差，仅对子空间分量计算线性层输出并缓存低秩权重，同时使用逐token门控机制决定是否计算残差校正，从而在可控误差范围内保持输出分布。在GPT-2 124M、GPT-J 6B、OPT 6.7B上，AMD MI300X实现线性层权重读取3.0-10.5倍加速，困惑度比低于1.00，top-1 token一致性超98%。在GPT-J 6B上(k=256, ε=0.05)可实现字符级完全一致的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语言模型推理时，线性层的权重读取带宽是关键瓶颈。作者利用每层token激活流形的低有效秩特性，旨在减少线性层权重读取量，加速推理的同时保持输出分布的可控精度。

Method: 1) 将每层激活向量分解为子空间分量（主成分）和残差；2) 对子空间分量，通过缓存低秩权重矩阵计算输出，减少内存带宽消耗；3) 设计逐token门控机制，根据是否超出容忍度ε决定是否计算残差校正，从而在保持输出分布精度的前提下跳过不必要的计算。

Result: 在AMD MI300X上，GPT-2 124M、GPT-J 6B、OPT 6.7B三个模型实现线性层权重读取3.0-10.5倍加速；困惑度比低于1.00，top-1 token一致性超过98%；在GPT-J 6B上(k=256, ε=0.05)达到字符级完全一致的输出。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效加速Transformer推理，且无需重训练、架构修改或对注意力机制做近似，具有实用性和通用性。

Abstract: A method is presented for accelerating inference in transformer language models by exploiting the low effective rank of the token activation manifold at each layer. The method decomposes each activation vector into a subspace component and a residual, computes the linear-layer output on the subspace component via a cached low-rank weight image at reduced memory bandwidth, and applies a per-token gate that determines whether the residual correction is computed or skipped. The gate ensures that the output distribution is preserved to within a controllable tolerance. Validation on three model families (GPT-2 124M, GPT-J 6B, OPT 6.7B) on AMD MI300X demonstrates effective speedups of 3.0x to 10.5x on linear-layer weight reads with perplexity ratios below 1.00 and top-1 token agreement above 98%. The method requires no retraining, no architectural modification, and no approximation of the attention mechanism. At the operating point (k = 256, ε = 0.05) on GPT-J 14 6B, the accelerated model produces character-for-character identical output to the baseline.

</details>


### [92] [Taming the Curses of Multiagency in Robust Markov Games with Large State Space through Linear Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03125)
*Jingchu Gai,Laixi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模（或无限）状态空间下的分布鲁棒马尔可夫博弈（RMG），在总变差距离定义的不确定集下，提出了首个打破多智能体维数灾难的样本高效算法，涵盖生成模型和在线交互两种场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL算法面临鲁棒性和数据效率的双重挑战。虽然分布鲁棒马尔可夫博弈（RMG）能提升鲁棒性，但当前可证明数据高效的RMG算法仅限于表格型设置（有限状态动作空间），无法处理大规模状态空间。唯一超越表格设置的工作使用线性函数近似（LFA），但受限于苛刻的极小值假设且仍受多智能体维数灾难困扰。

Method: 本文针对总变差距离定义的不确定集，在生成模型设置和新提出的在线交互设置下，结合线性函数近似（LFA）开发了可证明数据高效的算法。

Result: 所提算法首次在大规模（可能无限）状态空间的RMG中打破了多智能体维数灾难对样本复杂度的束缚，且这一结果不依赖于不确定集的具体构造方式。

Conclusion: 本文为大规模状态空间下分布鲁棒MARL提供了首个打破维数灾难的样本高效算法，填补了该领域的重要理论空白。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) holds great potential but faces robustness challenges due to environmental uncertainty. To address this, distributionally robust Markov games (RMGs) optimize worst-case performance when the environment deviates from the nominal model within a uncertainty set. Beyond robustness, an equally urgent goal for MARL is data efficiency -- sampling from vast state and action spaces that grow exponentially with the number of agents potentially leads to the curse of multiagency. However, current provably data-efficient algorithms for RMGs are limited to tabular settings with finite state and action spaces, which are only computationally manageable for small-scale problems, leaving RMGs with large-scale (or infinite) state spaces largely unexplored. The only existing work beyond tabular settings focuses on linear function approximation (LFA) for a restrictive class of RMGs using vanish minimal value assumption and still suffers from sample complexity with the curse of multiagency. In this work, we focuses on general RMGs with LFA. For uncertainty sets defined by total variation distance, we develop provably data-efficient algorithms that break the curse of multiagency in both the generative model setting and a newly proposed online interactive setting. To our knowledge, our results are the first to break the curse of multiagency of sample complexity for RMGs with large (possibly infinite) state spaces, regardless of the uncertainty set construction.

</details>


### [93] [Instance-Level Costs for Nuanced Classifier Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03135)
*Kabir Kang,Stephen Mussmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出归一化超额成本（NEC）指标，对分类错误按示例成本加权，发现模型错误集中在低成本的模糊样本上，但成本感知训练仅在成本可从输入特征预测时有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分类对所有错误一视同仁，但在内容审核、医疗筛查等安全关键应用中，明显案例的错误代价远高于模糊案例，因此需要能反映实例级不同成本的评估指标。

Method: 提出NEC指标，将分类错误按每个示例的成本加权（成本可来自标注者投票差距、决策阈值距离或置信度评分），当成本均匀时退化为标准错误率。在文本、图像和表格基准上评估，并测试了损失加权、采样策略和回归等成本感知训练方法。

Result: NEC通常远低于错误率（如5%错误率模型可达1.8% NEC），表明错误集中在低成本的模糊样本上。但成本感知训练仅在成本可从输入特征预测时（合成数据）有稳定提升，在真实数据集上效果混合或可忽略。

Conclusion: 提供了导出和评估实例级误分类成本的实用方法论，即使成本感知训练收益有限，NEC指标本身也能揭示模型错误分布的关键特征。

Abstract: Standard classification treats all errors equally, but in content moderation, medical screening, and safety-critical applications, mistakes on clear-cut cases are far more costly than errors on ambiguous ones. We propose normalized excess cost (NEC), a metric that weights classification errors by per-example costs and reduces to standard error rate when costs are uniform. Costs can derive from annotator vote margins, distance from decision thresholds, or confidence ratings. Across text, image, and tabular benchmarks, we find that NEC is often substantially lower than error rate -- models with 5\% error rate can achieve 1.8\% NEC -- revealing that most mistakes concentrate on ambiguous, low-cost examples. However, incorporating costs into training via loss weighting, sampling strategies, or regression yields inconsistent benefits: improvements appear only when costs are predictable from input features, as in our synthetic control, while real-world datasets show mixed or negligible gains. Our framework provides a practical methodology for deriving and evaluating instance-level misclassification costs, even when cost-sensitive training offers limited benefit.

</details>


### [94] [OCRR: A Benchmark for Online Correction Recovery under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03153)
*Adrian Grassi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OCRR基准，用于评估分类系统在分布偏移下的在线纠错恢复速度。实验表明，所提出的Substrate方法在保持原始分布准确率的同时恢复新类别准确率，显著优于现有持续学习方法、LoRA微调及在线学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态基准无法衡量系统在分布偏移（新类别、查询改写等）下通过用户纠错流进行在线恢复的速度，缺乏相关评估框架。

Method: 提出OCRR基准，流式处理语料库，对错误预测施加oracle或随机纠错，报告新类别准确率和原始分布准确率随纠错次数的变化曲线。评估了9种基线算法（来自5个类别）及7种有界存储变体，包括标准在线学习、持续学习、检索/参数混合、参数高效微调（LoRA on DeBERTa-v3-large）和基于哈希链的追加式Substrate。

Result: 在Banking77和CLINC150上，Substrate在oracle和稀疏纠错策略下，新类别准确率恢复至88.7±2.9%，原始分布准确率保持95.4±0.8%，在同等内存预算下超过最佳持续学习基线32.6个百分点，超过LoRA微调84.6个百分点。分类准确率在99%时保持稳定，即使ANN召回率@5从0.69降至0.23。

Conclusion: Substrate的边际带多数投票机制对检索不完美具有鲁棒性，纯top-k召回率指标无法预测这一特性。代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Static benchmarks measure a model frozen at training time. Real systems face distribution shift: new categories, paraphrased queries, drift: and must recover online via user corrections. No existing benchmark measures recovery speed under correction streams. We introduce OCRR (Online Correction Recovery Rate): a benchmark that streams a corpus through a classification system, applies oracle or stochastic corrections to wrong predictions, and reports two curves: novel-class accuracy and original-distribution accuracy versus correction count. We evaluate the substrate alongside nine baseline algorithms from five families plus seven bounded-storage variants of the substrate for the Pareto sweep, including standard online-learning baselines (river), continual-learning methods (EWC, A-GEM, LwF), retrieval/parametric hybrids (kNN-LM), parameter-efficient fine-tuning of a 1.5 B-parameter encoder (LoRA on DeBERTa-v3-large), and a hash-chained append-only substrate (Substrate). On Banking77 and CLINC150, under oracle and sparse correction policies, the substrate is the only system that simultaneously recovers novel-class accuracy (88.7 +/- 2.9 %) and retains original-distribution accuracy (95.4 +/- 0.8 %) beating the next-best published continual-learning baseline by 32.6 percentage points at equal memory budget, and beating LoRA-on-DeBERTa-v3-large by 84.6 percentage points on retention. We further find that classification accuracy remains stable at 99 % even as approximate-nearest-neighbour recall@5 degrades from 0.69 to 0.23 across 10 k to 10 M corpus scales, suggesting the substrate's margin-band majority vote is robust to retrieval imperfection in a way that pure top-k recall metrics do not predict. Code and data are available at https://github.com/adriangrassi/ocrr-benchmark.

</details>


### [95] [Pairwise matrices for sparse autoencoders: single-feature inspection mislabels causal axes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03160)
*Michael A. Riegler,Birk Sebastian Frostelid Torpmann-Hagen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“成对矩阵协议”（pairwise matrix protocol），通过联合调控特征系数发现标准稀疏自编码器（SAE）单特征协议遗漏的三类现象：1）特征呈倒U型响应而非单调；2）近正交簇特征联合抑制损伤非相关领域的组合推理；3）联合抑制产生占位文本，且相干性损失依赖于扰动方向模式而非幅度。这些发现在Gemma-2-2B-it上复现，并定位了Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct中的因果特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAE可解释性协议仅对每个特征在其最高激活上下文中标注标签，并通过单特征 steering 验证，这种方法可能遗漏特征之间交互作用以及非线性响应模式，需要更全面的分析协议。

Method: 提出成对矩阵协议，联合调控多个特征的 steering 系数（共变条件），对Qwen3-1.7B-Instruct进行系统分析，并在Gemma-2-2B-it上复现，在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上定位因果特征。

Result: 1）AI自我免责特征呈倒U型响应，中等系数切换为哲学式叙述；2）近正交簇特征联合抑制（c=-500）同时损坏内省提示、食谱和引擎解释的组合输出；3）等几何比较显示单特征→策略性填充，随机方向→多样内容，联合抑制→占位文本，相干性损失取决于方向模式而非幅度。

Conclusion: 标准单特征协议不足以捕捉特征的交互效应和非线性行为，成对矩阵协议揭示了更丰富的因果结构，且所有发现跨模型可复现，提升了SAE可解释性的可靠性。

Abstract: The standard sparse-autoencoder (SAE) interpretability protocol labels each feature from its top-activating contexts and validates by single-feature steering. We propose the pairwise matrix protocol, co-varying steering coefficient with joint condition, and report three findings the standard one-corner protocol misses on Qwen3-1.7B-Instruct, replicated on Gemma-2-2B-it. First, a feature labelled "AI self-disclaimer" from its top contexts produces an inverted U-shape under a coefficient sweep: at c=+500 the model substitutes a fluent contemplative-philosopher voice for the disclaimer. Two further features anchor the criterion (one monotonic, one pure breakdown). Second, three near-orthogonal cluster-specific features that individually steer a philosophy-of-mind register, jointly suppressed at c=-500, damage grounded composition on recipes and engine explanations as well as introspective prompts; single-feature suppression at the same magnitude leaves controls intact. Third, a matched-geometry comparison of single-feature, joint, and random-direction perturbations (norm ~1.55, cosine ~0.64) yields three distinct output regimes: single-feature substitutes strategy filler, random direction substitutes diverse content, joint suppression alone produces placeholder text. Coherence loss is direction-pattern-dependent, not magnitude-dependent. All three findings reproduce on Gemma with model-specific damage signatures; the matched-geometry control is CI-separated by ~10x. The pipeline also locates a top causally responsible feature in Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct.

</details>


### [96] [Global and Local Topology-Aware Attention with Persistent Homology and Euler Biases for Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03163)
*Usef Faghihi,Amir Saki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种拓扑感知注意力框架，通过持久同调、欧拉特征变换和核希尔伯特通道将几何结构注入注意力计算。采用验证门控残差机制，在无泄漏协议下评估三种架构（轻量Attention/Ridge、PatchTST、TimeSeriesTransformer），在合成基准和真实数据集（CO2、S&P 500、NASA轴承退化）上验证，结果表明拓扑作为归纳偏置能显著提升预测性能（RMSE降低12.5%-47.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准点积注意力机制无法显式表示科学时间序列中蕴含的预测性几何结构（如连通性、循环、壳状几何、方向变化、非线性邻域），因此需要引入拓扑感知机制来增强注意力表达力。

Method: 提出拓扑感知注意力框架：使用持久同调（H0-H2）、锚定欧拉特征变换、核希尔伯特通道增强注意力logits；设计验证门控局部残差（含Zeng式局部H0分量），仅在验证数据支持时才激活校正；采用无泄漏协议（仅训练集校准、仅验证集选择、仅测试集报告），比较精确Vietoris-Rips计算与平滑拓扑替代方案。

Result: 在189个配对单元上评估：轻量Attention/Ridge 63单元中46个正向效果，RMSE降低12.5%（p=7.2e-4）；PatchTST 33个正向、20个持平，降低23.5%（p=3.5e-5）；TimeSeriesTransformer 47个正向，降低47.8%（p<1e-4）。

Conclusion: 拓扑可作为验证集选择的、架构兼容的归纳偏置，在几何结构可预测时显著提升时间序列预测性能，但效果存在异构性，依赖于数据集和架构。

Abstract: Scientific time series often encode predictive geometric structure, including connectivity, cycles, shell-like geometry, directional changes, and nonlinear neighborhoods, that standard dot-product attention does not explicitly represent. We introduce a topology-aware attention framework that adds such structure to attention logits using persistent homology (H0-H2), anchored Euler characteristic transforms, and kernel-Hilbert channels. A validation-gated local residual captures local topological signals, including a Zeng-style local H0 component, only when held-out validation data support the correction. Exact Vietoris-Rips computations and smooth topological surrogates are evaluated under a no-leakage protocol with train-only calibration, validation-only selection, and test-only reporting.
  We evaluate guarded topology-aware variants across three architecture families: lightweight attention/Ridge, PatchTSTForRegression, and TimeSeriesTransformerForPrediction. Experiments include synthetic benchmarks isolating higher-order topology and real datasets covering CO2, S&P 500 return-window geometry, and NASA IMS bearing degradation. The audit uses matched paired comparisons across seven dataset units, three random seeds, and three chronological splits, giving 63 paired units per architecture and 189 paired units overall. Topology-aware models show positive paired effects when geometry is predictive, with heterogeneous magnitude across datasets and architectures. Lightweight attention/Ridge improves in 46 of 63 units, with mean relative RMSE reduction of 12.5% and paired randomization p=7.2e-4; PatchTST improves in 33 units and retains the baseline in 20 units, with 23.5% reduction and p=3.5e-5; and TimeSeriesTransformer improves in 47 units, with 47.8% reduction and p<1e-4. The results support topology as a validation-selected, architecture-compatible inductive bias.

</details>


### [97] [Moral Sensitivity in LLMs: A Tiered Evaluation of Contextual Bias via Behavioral Profiling and Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03217)
*Yash Aggarwal,Atmika Gorti,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Krishnaprasad Thirunarayan,Manas Gaur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出道德敏感性指数(MSI)，通过七级压力测试量化LLM的偏差输出概率，并结合行为分析与机械验证揭示偏差涌现机制。研究发现偏差呈现U型曲线：小模型偏差强，指令微调消除偏差，推理蒸馏重新引入偏差至小模型水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM偏差评估采用二元分类（"有偏/无偏"），忽略了偏差涌现的渐变性和上下文敏感性，无法捕捉模型在细微情境下的道德推理差异。

Method: 两阶段方法：1) 行为 profiling阶段，提出MSI指标，通过从抽象数值问题到历史社会经济不公正场景的七级压力测试量化偏差概率；2) 机械验证阶段，选取刑事偏差场景作为探针，结合logit lens、注意力分析、激活修补和语义探针对横跨三个能力层级的六种模型进行电路级分析。

Result: 发现了偏差U型曲线：小语言模型表现出强刑事偏差；扩大至指令微调模型后偏差消除；推理蒸馏模型在参数数量相同的情况下将偏差重新引入至小模型水平。Gemini 1.5在第五级社会经济框架下MSI达72.7%，Claude表现出与身份基础安全训练一致的尖锐抑制。

Conclusion: 推理蒸馏压缩了推理轨迹，以重新激活浅层统计关联的方式引入偏差，且社会性负载线索同时驱动高MSI分数和机械验证中识别出的偏差回路，提供了跨阶段验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in settings that require nuanced ethical reasoning, yet existing bias evaluations treat model outputs as simply "biased" or "unbiased." This binary framing misses the gradual, context-sensitive way bias actually emerges. We address this gap in two stages: behavioral profiling and mechanistic validation. In the behavioral stage, we introduce the Moral Sensitivity Index (MSI), a metric that quantifies the probability of biased output across a graduated, seven-tier stress test ranging from abstract numerical problems to scenarios rooted in historical and socioeconomic injustice. Evaluating four leading models (Claude 3.5, Qwen 3.5, Llama 3, and Gemini 1.5), we identify distinct behavioral signatures shaped by alignment design: for instance, Gemini 1.5 reaches 72.7% MSI by Tier 5 under socioeconomic framing, while Claude exhibits sharp suppression consistent with identity-based safety training. We then verify these behavioral patterns mechanistically. We select criminal-bias scenarios, which produced the highest MSI scores across models, as probes and apply logit lens, attention analysis, activation patching, and semantic probing to a controlled set of six models spanning three capability tiers: small language models (SLMs), instruction-tuned base models, and reasoning-distilled variants. Circuit-level analysis reveals a U-curve of bias: SLMs exhibit strong criminal bias; scaling to instruction-tuned models eliminates it; reasoning distillation reintroduces bias to SLM-like levels despite identical parameter counts, suggesting distillation compresses reasoning traces in ways that reactivate shallow statistical associations. Critically, the socially loaded cues that drive high MSI scores activate the same bias-driving circuits identified mechanistically, providing cross-stage validation.

</details>


### [98] [Self-Mined Hardness for Safety Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03226)
*Prakhar Gupta,Garv Shah,Donghua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需精心设计对抗数据集的安全微调方法：通过评估模型自身 rollout 的 harmful 频率来给提示难度打分，然后用最难提示+模型非越狱 rollout 进行微调。在 Llama-3-8B-Instruct 和 Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct 上，该方法将 WildJailbreak 攻击成功率从 11.5%/20.1% 降至 1-3%，但将拒绝率从 14-22% 飙升至 74-94%。通过将硬提示与对抗性框架化的良性提示（外表像越狱但意图良性的提示）1:1 交织，可将拒绝率降至 30-51%（8B）和 52-72%（3B），攻击成功率仅上升 2-6 个百分点。在混合训练中，选择最难的一半提示而非随机一半，可将剩余攻击成功率再降低 35-50%（约 3 个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安全微调需要精心策划的对抗数据集，成本高昂且泛化性有限。本文旨在探索一种无需手动构造对抗样本、直接利用模型自身评分来自动筛选困难提示的安全微调方法。

Method: (1) 对每个候选提示，通过目标模型自身 rollout 被判断为 harmful 的频率来量化提示难度；(2) 选择最难提示，配对模型自身非越狱（non-jailbroken）rollout 进行微调；(3) 进一步引入1:1比例与对抗性框架化良性提示交织训练，以及采用 hardest-half 采样策略优化混合训练效果。

Result: 在 Llama-3-8B-Instruct 上 ASR 从 11.5% 降至 1-3%，但拒绝率从 14-22% 升至 74-94%；交织训练后拒绝率降至 30-51%（8B）/52-72%（3B），ASR 仅上升 2-6 个百分点；hardest-half 策略比随机 half 进一步降低 ASR 35-50%。

Conclusion: 基于模型自身评分自动筛选困难提示的安全微调是有效的，但会带来过度拒绝问题；通过交织对抗性框架化良性提示可缓解过度拒绝，且 hardest-half 采样优于随机采样。该方法在减少人工数据依赖的同时实现了更强的安全性和可控的拒绝率。

Abstract: Safety fine-tuning of language models typically requires a curated adversarial dataset. We take a different approach: score each candidate prompt's difficulty by how often the target model's own rollouts are judged harmful, then fine-tune on the hardest prompts paired with the model's own non-jailbroken rollouts. On Llama-3-8B-Instruct and Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, this approach cuts the WildJailbreak attack success rate from 11.5% and 20.1% down to 1-3%, but pushes refusal on jailbreak-shaped benign prompts from 14-22% to 74-94%. Interleaving the same hard prompts 1:1 with adversarially-framed benign prompts (prompts that look like jailbreaks but have benign intent) cuts that refusal back down to 30-51% on 8B and 52-72% on 3B, at a cost of 2-6 percentage points of attack success rate. Within the mixed regime, training on the hardest half of the eligible pool rather than a random half cuts the remaining ASR by 35-50% (about 3 percentage points) on both models.

</details>


### [99] [Text-Conditional JEPA for Learning Semantically Rich Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03245)
*Chen Huang,Xianhang Li,Vimal Thilak,Etai Littwin,Josh Susskind*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TC-JEPA 提出使用图像描述（文本）作为条件来减少图像自监督学习中掩码特征预测的不确定性，通过细粒度文本调节器将文本信息注入预测的补丁特征中，从而学习更具语义意义的视觉表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 I-JEPA 在掩码位置的特征预测面临视觉不确定性（同一位置可能有多种合理视觉特征），导致难以学习语义化的表征。

Method: 使用图像标题（文本）作为条件，通过细粒度文本调节器在输入文本令牌上计算稀疏交叉注意力，调制预测的补丁特征，使特征预测变得依赖于文本而更可预测。

Result: TC-JEPA 在下游任务性能和训练稳定性上均有提升，具有良好的扩展性；在多种任务上优于对比学习方法，尤其是在需要细粒度视觉理解和推理的任务上。

Conclusion: TC-JEPA 提出了一种基于特征预测的新型视觉-语言预训练范式，有效缓解了视觉不确定性带来的表征学习困难。

Abstract: Image-based Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (I-JEPA) offers a promising approach to visual self-supervised learning through masked feature prediction. However with the inherent visual uncertainty at masked positions, feature prediction remains challenging and may fail to learn semantic representations. In this work, we propose Text-Conditional JEPA (TC-JEPA) that uses image captions to reduce the prediction uncertainty. Specifically, we modulate the predicted patch features using a fine-grained text conditioner that computes sparse cross-attention over input text tokens. With such conditioning, patch features become predictable as a function of text, thus are more semantically meaningful. We show TC-JEPA improves downstream performance and training stability, with promising scaling properties. TC-JEPA also offers a new vision-language pretraining paradigm based on feature prediction only, outperforming contrastive methods on diverse tasks, especially those requiring fine-grained visual understanding and reasoning.

</details>


### [100] [Posterior-First Neural PDE Simulation: Inferring Hidden Problem State from a Single Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03247)
*Wenshuo Wang,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"后验优先的神经PDE模拟"框架：先推断最小任务充分问题状态的后验分布，再基于该后验进行条件预测。理论证明了贝叶斯下游价值可通过该后验分解、确定性坍塌存在模糊障碍，实验中后验恢复将池化nRMSE从0.175降至0.132，缩小了59.4%的直接到预言机差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经PDE模拟器在部署时通常仅接收单一观测场，传统的场到未来预测器会将不同的潜在问题状态坍缩到相同的确定性界面，丢失了可靠 rollout 和下游决策所需的模糊性。作者希望解决这一信息丢失问题。

Method: 提出后验优先的神经PDE模拟框架：第一步推断最小任务充分问题状态的后验分布，第二步基于该后验进行条件预测。理论层面建立了贝叶斯下游价值通过后验分解、通过适当评分规则使细化标签可学习、确定性坍缩存在模糊障碍等结论。

Result: 在合成精确模糊实验中，点估计与后验估计的差距追踪了理论预测的障碍。在元数据隐藏的PDEBench任务上，后验恢复将池化rollout nRMSE从0.175降至0.132，缩小了59.4%的直接到预言机差距。

Conclusion: 单观测神经PDE模拟应采用后验优先范式而非整体场到未来的预测方法。

Abstract: Neural PDE simulators often receive only a single observed field at deployment. In this setting, a field-to-future predictor can collapse distinct latent problem states into the same deterministic interface, losing the ambiguity needed for reliable rollout and downstream decisions. We propose posterior-first neural PDE simulation: first infer a posterior over the minimal task-sufficient problem state, then condition prediction on that posterior. The resulting theory connects the object, the learning target, and the failure mode: Bayes downstream values factor through this posterior, refinement labels make it learnable by proper scoring rules, and deterministic collapse incurs an ambiguity barrier whenever the true posterior is non-Dirac. Synthetic exact-ambiguity experiments show that point-versus-posterior gaps track the predicted barrier. On metadata-hidden PDEBench tasks, posterior recovery reduces pooled rollout nRMSE from 0.175 to 0.132, closing 59.4% of the direct-to-oracle gap. These results suggest that single-observation neural PDE simulation should be posterior-first rather than monolithic field-to-future prediction.

</details>


### [101] [Ortho-Hydra: Orthogonalized Experts for DiT LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03252)
*Seunghyun Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散Transformer（DiT）在多风格数据上微调LoRA时出现的"风格渗漏"问题，本文提出Ortho-Hydra方法，通过Cayley正交共享基与专家不相交输出子空间的重参数化，解决了HydraLoRA中路由器在零初始化下无法打破均匀先验的冷启动死锁问题，使专家专业化在训练初期即可发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA微调扩散Transformer处理多风格数据时存在风格渗漏（style bleed）问题——单一低秩残差无法同时表示多个不同的艺术家风格特征，优化器倾向于收敛到这些特征的均值。HydraLoRA式的混合专家LoRA虽然用路由器+多个专家头来缓解此问题，但零初始化导致路由器从每个专家接收相同梯度，始终停留在均匀先验，专家对称演化，最终退化为$E$倍成本的单秩$r$ LoRA。

Method: 提出Ortho-Hydra重参数化方案：结合OFT风格的Cayley正交共享基（Cayley-orthogonal shared basis）与每个专家的不相交输出子空间（disjoint output subspaces），后者从预训练权重的top-$(Er)$左奇异向量中划分得到。不相交性使得路由器在每个专家上的得分在训练第0步就是非退化的，因此在任何专家训练之前就获得了梯度信号，打破了冷启动死锁。

Result: 在DiT流水线上对比两种HydraLoRA基线（零初始化的共享基变体、原始$\sigma=0.1$高斯抖动缓解方法）与Ortho-Hydra。两种基线在前1000步内均无法离开均匀先验；Ortho-Hydra在几百步内就开始去均匀化（de-uniformising）。端到端生成质量不在本文讨论范围内，主要报告构建方法、冷启动机制及其改变的路由动态。

Conclusion: Ortho-Hydra通过正交共享基和不相交输出子空间的重参数化，有效解决了混合专家LoRA中路由器冷启动死锁问题，使专家能在训练早期开始专业化分工，避免了退化为单LoRA的高成本低效训练。代码已开源。

Abstract: LoRA fine-tuning of diffusion transformers (DiT) on multi-style data suffers from \emph{style bleed}: a single low-rank residual cannot represent several distinct artist fingerprints, and the optimizer converges to their average. Mixture-of-experts LoRA in the HydraLoRA style replaces the up-projection with $E$ heads under a router, but when every expert is zero-initialized the router receives identical gradient from each head and remains at the uniform prior. The experts then evolve permutation-symmetrically, and the network trains as a single rank-$r$ LoRA at $E{\times}$ the cost. We present \textbf{Ortho-Hydra}, a re-parameterisation that combines an OFT-style Cayley-orthogonal shared basis with per-expert \emph{disjoint output subspaces} carved from the top-$(Er)$ left singular vectors of the pretrained weight. Disjointness makes the router's per-expert score non-degenerate at step~$0$, so specialization receives gradient signal before any expert has trained. We test the predicted deadlock on a DiT pipeline by comparing two HydraLoRA baselines, a zero-initialized shared-basis variant and the original $σ{=}0.1$ Gaussian-jitter mitigation, against Ortho-Hydra under a matched optimiser, dataset, and step budget. Neither baseline leaves the uniform prior within the first $1\text{k}$ steps; Ortho-Hydra begins de-uniformising within the first few hundred. End-task generation quality on multi-style data is out of scope; we report the construction, the cold-start mechanism, and the routing dynamics it changes. Code: https://github.com/sorryhyun/anima_lora.

</details>


### [102] [Do LLMs have core beliefs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03255)
*Anna Sokol,Marianna B. Ganapini,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究大型语言模型（LLMs）是否具备类似人类的"核心信念"（core commitments）。通过设计名为"对抗性对话树"（ADTs）的探测框架，在科学、历史、地理、生物学和数学五个领域进行测试，发现大多数LLM无法维持稳定的世界观。尽管部分较新模型稳定性有所提升，但在对话压力下最终仍会放弃关键信念。结果表明模型在论证能力上有所进步，但所有当前模型都缺乏人类认知的关键组成部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关于LLM是否具备人类级认知的辩论中，很少有研究关注人类认知的一个结构性组成部分：核心信念——即那些为构建世界观提供基础的、通常抵制被否定的真理。本文旨在探究LLM是否持有类似的核心承诺。

Method: 提出一种名为"对抗性对话树"（Adversarial Dialogue Trees, ADTs）的探测框架，在科学、历史、地理、生物学和数学五个领域对多个LLM进行测试，通过对话压力考察模型是否能维持稳定的核心信念。

Result: 大多数LLM无法维持稳定的世界观；部分较新模型在稳定性上有所改进，但在持续的对话压力下最终仍会放弃关键承诺。

Conclusion: 模型在论证能力上随代际有所提升，但所有当前模型均缺乏人类认知中维持核心信念这一关键组成部分。

Abstract: The rise of Large Language Models (LLMs) has sparked debate about whether these systems exhibit human-level cognition. In this debate, little attention has been paid to a structural component of human cognition: core beliefs, truths that provide a foundation around which we can build a worldview. These commitments usually resist debunking, as abandoning them would represent a fundamental shift in how we see reality. In this paper, we ask whether LLMs hold anything akin to core commitments. Using a probing framework we call Adversarial Dialogue Trees (ADTs) over five domains (science, history, geography, biology, and mathematics), we find that most LLMs fail to maintain a stable worldview. Though some recent models showed improved stability, they still eventually failed to maintain key commitments under conversational pressure. These results document an improvement in argumentative skills across model generations but indicate that all current models lack a key component of human-level cognition.

</details>


### [103] [A Universal Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space from Polynomial Alignment and IMQ Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03262)
*Taha Bouhsine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种有理隐单元核函数Yat kernel，兼具PSD性、普适性、特征性和严格正定性，并通过偏置的有限差分精确重构IMQ核，支持有限可训练中心展开与显式泛化界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统核方法中径向基函数（如IMQ）缺乏非径向对齐通道，限制了其在方向敏感任务中的表达能力。作者希望构造一种兼具普适性与代数可操作性的核函数，能够在保持正定性的同时引入方向性对齐机制，并通过有限差分操作精确恢复IMQ核，从而统一两类核的优势。

Method: 提出Yat核 $k_{b,\varepsilon}(\mathbf{w},\mathbf{x}) = \frac{(\mathbf{w}^\top\mathbf{x}+b)^2}{\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{w}\|^2+\varepsilon}$，证明其当$b\ge0$时为PSD核，$b>0$时在Loewner序下主导缩放IMQ核，具有固定核普适性、特征性和严格正定性。推导方向远场迹$T_\infty g_\varepsilon(\cdot;\mathbf{w},b)(\mathbf{u})=(\mathbf{u}^\top\mathbf{w})^2$以体现非径向对齐。通过偏置的二次有限差分精确恢复IMQ核，证明在任意维度上三个Yat原子即可实现逐点精确相等。利用共享参数$(b,\varepsilon)$的Yat层实现有限中心展开，给出闭式RKHS范数和显式Rademacher泛化界。

Result: Yat核在$b>0$时普适且特征，严格正定；方向远场迹揭示其非径向对齐能力；三个正偏置Yat原子通过二次有限差分可精确重构任意IMQ原子；有限中心展开具有闭式范数和基于对角项$(\|\mathbf{x}\|^2+b)^2/\varepsilon$的Rademacher泛化界。

Conclusion: Yat核统一了有理核与径向基核的优势，在保持普适性和特征性的同时引入了可解释的方向对齐机制，为核方法提供了一种兼具表达力与理论保障的新型隐藏单元原语。

Abstract: We introduce the Yat kernel $$k_{b,\varepsilon}(\mathbf{w},\mathbf{x})=\frac{(\mathbf{w}^\top\mathbf{x}+b)^2}{\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{w}\|^2+\varepsilon},\qquad b\ge 0,\ \varepsilon>0,$$ a rational hidden-unit primitive whose units are Mercer sections over a shared input/weight space. For $b\ge 0$ the kernel is PSD; for $b>0$ it dominates a scaled inverse-multiquadric (IMQ) in the Loewner order, yielding fixed-kernel universality, characteristicness, and strict positive definiteness on every compact domain. The polynomial numerator opens nonradial alignment channels absent from finite IMQ expansions, witnessed by the directional far-field trace $T_\infty g_\varepsilon(\cdot;\mathbf{w},b)(\mathbf{u})=(\mathbf{u}^\top\mathbf{w})^2$. Algebraically, a second finite difference in the bias recovers any IMQ atom from three positive-bias Yat atoms exactly, sharp at three atoms in every dimension at exact pointwise equality. A trained shared-$(b,\varepsilon)$ Yat layer is therefore a finite learned-center expansion in a fixed universal characteristic RKHS, with closed-form norm $\boldsymbolα^\top\mathbf{K}\boldsymbolα$ and explicit diagonal $(\|\mathbf{x}\|^2+b)^2/\varepsilon$ driving a Rademacher generalization bound.

</details>


### [104] [Partially Observed Structural Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03268)
*Turan Orujlu,Jordan Matelsky,Martin V. Butz,Charley M. Wu,Konrad P. Kording*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出部分观测结构因果模型（POSCMs），将潜在上下文对因果系统的交互结构和下游机制的共同影响形式化，并通过Kolmogorov-Arnold-Sprecher边函数分解实现边干预，在视网膜模拟器中验证了结构-机制混杂的可识别性理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构因果模型（SCMs）假设图结构已知或固定，无法处理潜在上下文同时影响因果图结构和变量机制的真实场景，且缺乏在边层级进行外科手术式干预的形式化框架。

Method: 提出POSCMs扩展SCMs，支持内源图结构；采用Kolmogorov-Arnold-Sprecher边函数分解将节点机制表示为父节点单变量函数之和，显式参数化二元功能贡献；建立可识别性理论明确所需干预族。

Result: 在虚拟人视网膜模拟器中：(i) 当上下文潜伏且无上下文干预时复现不可识别性；(ii) 仅观察节点干预时出现结构-机制混杂；(iii) 通过目标节点干预恢复突触输入-输出关系，验证了正核可识别性结论。

Conclusion: POSCMs将SCMs推广至更贴近现实世界的因果推理场景，为处理图结构与机制耦合的因果系统提供了理论框架和实用工具。

Abstract: Here we introduce Partially Observed Structural Causal Models (POSCMs) that formalize causal systems where latent contexts co-determine both the interaction structure and downstream mechanisms on observed variables. POSCMs provide an extension of structural causal models (SCMs), as a self-contained causal modeling framework for endogenous graphs, allowing for an intervention hierarchy spanning node- and edge-level context and endogenous variable interventions. To enable surgical edge interventions, we adopt a Kolmogorov-Arnold-Sprecher edge-functional decomposition, an existence theorem for representing each node mechanism as a sum of univariate functions of its parents, yielding an explicit parametrization of dyadic functional contributions. We provide an identifiability theory that clarifies which intervention families would suffice to disentangle structure formation from mechanisms. We empirically validate these predictions in a biophysically detailed virtual human retina simulator, constructing intervention protocols that (i) reproduce the non-identifiability predicted when context is latent and no context-level interventions are available, (ii) exhibit structure-mechanism confounding under latent edges when only node interventions are observed, and (iii) recover synaptic input-output relationships via targeted node interventions, consistent with our positive kernel identifiability result. Our work generalizes SCMs in a way that allows it to work in a world closer to the one we live in.

</details>


### [105] [Distributed Learning with Adversarial Gradient Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03313)
*Nawapon Sangsiri,Yufei Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究分布式学习中客户端返回受扰梯度信息的学习问题，针对凸且L-光滑函数在对抗性梯度扰动下的学习，提出了最优次优间隙的紧致可行阈值，并给出了达到该阈值且具有可证明查询复杂度保证的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式学习中的隐私担忧导致客户端可能故意返回被篡改的梯度信息，本文旨在探究在这种对抗性梯度扰动下，理论上能达到的最小次优间隙以及所需的最少查询次数。

Method: 理论分析了凸且L-光滑函数在梯度扰动有界距离下的学习问题，建立了次优间隙的紧致可行阈值，并设计了达到这些阈值的算法，同时提供了查询复杂度的理论保证。

Result: 得到了次优间隙的紧致可行阈值，以及达到这些阈值所需的查询复杂度上界。

Conclusion: 本文为对抗性梯度扰动下的分布式学习提供了理论极限和最优算法，填补了该问题在凸光滑函数设定下的理论基础。

Abstract: Privacy concerns in distributed learning often lead clients to return intentionally altered gradient information. We consider the problem of learning convex and $L$-smooth functions under adversarial gradient perturbation, where a client's gradient reply to a server query can deviate arbitrarily from the true gradient subject to a distance bound. Our study focuses on two fundamental questions: (i) what is the smallest achievable sub-optimality gap (i.e., excess error in optimization) under such responses, and (ii) how many queries are sufficient to guarantee a given sub-optimality gap? We establish tight feasibility thresholds on the sub-optimality gap and provide algorithms that achieve these thresholds with provable query complexity guarantees.

</details>


### [106] [DGPO: Distribution Guided Policy Optimization for Fine Grained Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03327)
*Hongbo Jin,Rongpeng Zhu,Zhongjing Du,Xu Jiang,Jingqi Tian,Qiaoman Zhang,Jiayu Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Distribution Guided Policy Optimization (DGPO)，一种无需critic的强化学习框架，通过将分布偏差重新解释为引导信号而非刚性惩罚，解决了序列级信用分配和KL散度梯度不稳定的问题，用于提升大语言模型的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习算法（如GRPO）存在两个关键问题：(1) 序列级信用分配过于粗粒度，难以在长链思维生成中隔离关键推理步骤；(2) 无界KL散度惩罚导致梯度不稳定和模式寻求保守主义，抑制了新颖推理轨迹的探索。

Method: 提出Distribution Guided Policy Optimization (DGPO)，一种无需critic的强化学习框架，核心创新是将分布偏差重新解释为引导信号而非刚性惩罚，从而缓解梯度不稳定并鼓励探索。

Result: 通过将分布偏差作为引导信号而非惩罚，改善了梯度稳定性，并促进了对新颖推理轨迹的探索，克服了GRPO等方法的局限性。

Conclusion: DGPO通过重新定义分布偏差的利用方式，为LLM在复杂推理任务上的强化学习对齐提供了更有效的替代方案，解决了信用分配和梯度稳定性的关键挑战。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is crucial for aligning large language models to perform complex reasoning tasks. However, current algorithms such as Group Relative Policy Optimization suffer from coarse grained, sequence level credit assignment, which severely struggles to isolate pivotal reasoning steps within long Chain of Thought generations. Furthermore, the standard unbounded Kullback Leibler divergence penalty induces severe gradient instability and mode seeking conservatism, ultimately stifling the discovery of novel reasoning trajectories. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Distribution Guided Policy Optimization, a novel critic free reinforcement learning framework that reinterprets distribution deviation as a guiding signal rather than a rigid penalty.

</details>


### [107] [LLM-ADAM: A Generalizable LLM Agent Framework for Pre-Print Anomaly Detection in Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03328)
*Ahmadreza Eslaminia,Chuhan Cai,Cameron Smith,Ruo-Syuan Mei,Shichen Li,Rajiv Malhotra,Klara Nahrstedt,Chenhui Shao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-ADAM，一种基于大语言模型(LLM)的增材制造预打印异常检测框架。该框架通过Extractor-LLM、Reference-LLM和Judge-LLM三个角色分解任务，将G代码映射为结构化参数、将打印机/材料文档转换为操作范围、并判定缺陷类别。在200个FFF G代码样本上，最佳配置达到87.5%准确率，远超单LLM基线的59.5%，证明结构化分解是性能提升的主要来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熔丝制造(FFF)技术虽使增材制造普及化，但将工艺规划责任转移给了缺乏制造专业知识的用户。语法有效的切片配置仍可能包含热或几何上的有害设置，细微的G代码编辑可能在打印开始前改变挤出、冷却或粘附。因此需要在打印前进行G代码筛查，在浪费材料和机器时间前捕获意外或恶意的机器程序错误。

Method: 提出LLM-ADAM框架，将预打印异常检测任务分解为三个角色：(1) Extractor-LLM将G代码文件映射为结构化工艺参数模式；(2) Reference-LLM将打印机和材料文档转换为对齐的操作范围；(3) Judge-LLM解释确定性偏差表和G代码证据，判定零件为非缺陷或属于异常类别。打印机、材料和LLM主干为可互换测试条件。

Result: 在N=200的FFF G代码语料库上评估，涵盖两种桌面打印机系列、两种材料和五个类别（非缺陷、欠挤出、过挤出、翘曲、拉丝）。最佳框架配置达到87.5%准确率，而最强的工程化单LLM基线仅为59.5%。缺陷类别识别接近或达到上限，残差错误集中在非缺陷样本上的保守误报。

Conclusion: 结构化分解而非主干模型强度是性能提升的主导来源。LLM-ADAM框架通过角色分工有效提升了预打印异常检测的准确率，证明了多LLM协作架构在增材制造工艺安全中的潜力。

Abstract: Additive manufacturing (AM) continues to transform modern manufacturing by enabling flexible, on-demand production of complex geometries across diverse industries. Fused filament fabrication (FFF) has extended AM to laboratories, classrooms, and small production environments, but this accessibility shifts process-planning responsibility to users who may lack manufacturing expertise. A syntactically valid slicer profile can still encode thermally or geometrically harmful settings, and subtle G-code edits can alter extrusion, cooling, or adhesion before a print begins. Pre-print G-code screening catches accidental or adversarial machine-program errors before material or machine time is wasted. This paper proposes LLM-ADAM as a generalizable LLM framework for pre-print anomaly detection in AM. The framework decomposes the task into three roles: Extractor-LLM maps a G-code file to a structured process-parameter schema; Reference-LLM converts printer and material documentation into aligned operating ranges; and Judge-LLM interprets a deterministic deviation table and G-code evidence to decide whether a part is non-defective or belongs to an anomaly class. Printers, materials, and LLM backbones are interchangeable test conditions, not fixed assumptions. We evaluate the framework on an N=200 FFF G-code corpus spanning two desktop printer families, two materials, and five classes including non-defective, under-extrusion, over-extrusion, warping, and stringing. The best framework configuration reaches 87.5% accuracy, compared with 59.5% for the strongest engineered single-LLM baseline. The results show that structured decomposition, rather than backbone strength alone, is the dominant source of improvement, with defect classes identified at or near ceiling for leading configurations while residual errors concentrate on conservative false alarms for non-defective samples.

</details>


### [108] [Toward Structural Multimodal Representations: Specialization, Selection, and Sparsification via Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03348)
